Abstract: We investigate a radially symmetric initial-boundary value problem whose separation of variables leads to a regular self-adjoint Sturm-Liouville problem with explicitly determined coefficients and a positive weight function. The resulting Sturm-Liouville operator is defined by a special geometry-induced coefficient pair that gives rise to a nonstandard weighted spectral structure. The associated radial operator possesses a discrete real spectrum and a complete orthonormal system of eigenfunctions in the corresponding weighted Hilbert space, yielding an exact spectral representation of the evolution problem. An exact transcendental spectral equation governing the eigenvalues is derived. To analyze the high-frequency regime, the Liouville-Green transformation is applied directly to the radial Sturm-Liouville equation. This yields explicit asymptotic formulas for the eigenvalues and eigenfunctions together with corresponding Liouville-Green quasimodes. Their weighted orthogonality properties and asymptotic completeness are established within the spectral framework of the exact operator. The resulting asymptotic spectral data are used to construct approximate solutions of the original boundary-value problem and to obtain error estimates for the spectral reconstruction. Numerical computations of the exact Sturm-Liouville spectrum show excellent agreement with the Liouville-Green predictions, thereby validating the asymptotic quantization formula and confirming the accuracy of the proposed spectral approximation.

</details>

Abstract: We formulate quantisation of gauge field theories on globally hyperbolic Lorentzian manifolds with marked hypersurfaces within the framework of perturbative algebraic quantum field theory (pAQFT) enriched by the Batalin, Fradkin, Vilkovisky formalism (BV/BFV). This allows one to incorporate some crucial aspects of the local functorial approach to gauge field theory on manifolds with boundary within the algebraic setting. In particular, we provide a pAQFT-formulation of the modified classical and quantum master equations (after Cattaneo, Mnev and Reshetikhin CMR), as well as a constructive way to build a renormalised quantum BFV operator correcting the failure of the Quantum Master Equation by means of boundary terms (in the appropriate sense). We find that the renormalised quantum homotopy dg Lie algebra arising from the Anomalous Master Ward Identity becomes curved when boundaries are considered. The failure of the quantum master equation is thus encoded by a nontrivial curvature term in an $L_\infty$ algebra. As a byproduct, we recover previous results of Hollands' on the relation between the (boundary) BRST charge and the BV operator, and we recover CMR's ansatz for the quantum BFV operator at leading perturbative order on causal cylinders in Abelian Yang--Mills theory.

Abstract: The causal action principle for causal fermion systems is analyzed for a minimizing measure whose support is assumed to have the structure of a smooth manifold $\tilde{M}$. Abstract: We study the non-equilibrium steady state of a weakly anharmonic chain of $N$ oscillators driven at its boundary by Langevin thermostats at unequal temperatures. Under a perturbative weak-anharmonicity condition, we prove a full-gradient logarithmic Sobolev inequality whose constant is independent of the chain length $N$. For homogeneous pinned chains, an additional quantitative regularity assumption yields a boundary space-time logarithmic Sobolev inequality and relative-entropy decay on the same $O(N^3)$ relaxation time scale as the harmonic chain. The proof extracts a finite-dimensional Gaussian component from the boundary noise and compares conditional terminal-state laws by a change of variables. The estimates are uniform over bounded positive temperatures and require no near-equilibrium assumption on their difference.

TL;DR: 本文从连续介质热力学角度重新审视了有限体积方法中真实流体的压力振荡问题，阐明了真实流体准保守（RFQC）方法的物理对应关系。RFQC方法通过沿路径线演化等熵内能-压力关系的仿射参数xi和E0来恢复机械平衡压力，而热力学重投影则将偏离等熵轨迹的部分转换为内能误差，从而保证热力学一致性和数值稳定性。进一步研究了RFQC方法的适用极限，在极端相变情形中发现了"液相迎风异常"（LUA）。理论分析表明，LUA是一种启动异常，可通过在初始间断处引入正则化策略来解决，使RFQC方法在极端热力学流动（如音速相变射流）中具有更高的精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从连续介质热力学视角重新审视有限体积方法中真实流体的压力振荡问题，并阐明RFQC方法的物理内涵及其适用极限。

Method: RFQC方法通过沿路径线演化等熵内能-压力关系的仿射参数xi和E0来恢复机械平衡压力，并利用热力学重投影将偏离等熵轨迹的偏差转换为内能误差。针对LUA，提出在初始间断处引入正则化策略。

Result: 识别了RFQC方法在极端相变情形中的液相迎风异常（LUA），揭示了其由相变时仿射斜率xi的数量级跳跃引发，导致下游低压单元压力恢复延迟并陷入低压循环的机制。

Conclusion: LUA是一种启动异常，通过引入初始间断正则化策略可解决，使RFQC方法在极端热力学流动中具备更高的精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: From the perspective of continuum thermodynamics, we revisit the pressure oscillation problem in finite-volume methods for real fluids and clarify the physical counterpart of the Real Fluid Quasi-Conservative (RFQC) method. The RFQC method recovers the mechanical-equilibrium pressure by evolving the affine parameters xi and E0 of the isentropic internal energy-pressure relation along pathlines, while the thermodynamic re-projection converts the deviation from the isentropic trajectory into an internal-energy error, thereby ensuring the thermodynamic consistency and numerical stability of the method. We then investigate the applicability limit of the RFQC method and identify a Liquid-upwind Anomaly (LUA) in extreme phase-change cases. For a Riemann problem involving liquid-vapor phase change, a numerical anomaly may occur if a liquid-upwind translational velocity is initially superimposed and exceeds a certain threshold. Theoretical analysis reveals that this anomaly is initiated by the orders-of-magnitude jump in the affine slope xi during phase change, which subsequently delays the pressure rise in the downstream low-pressure cell. Concurrently, the re-projection equivalently removes the positive pressure increment. As a result, a large re-projection internal-energy error is repeatedly generated in this anomalous cell, and the cell is trapped in a cycle of delayed low-pressure recovery. The analysis indicates that the LUA is a start-up anomaly, which can be resolved by introducing a regularization strategy at the initial discontinuity. With the proposed regularization strategy, the RFQC method is equipped with enhanced accuracy and robustness for extreme thermodynamic flows, such as sonic phase-change jets.

</details>

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯时空模型，用于整合被动监测（区域级汇总）和主动监测（医院级）数据，解决空间错位问题，以更准确估计传染病（如钩端螺旋体病）的真实疾病负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 扩展了Poisson-logistic框架，纳入就医概率的空间异质性，并考虑主动监测数据中诊断检测的不完美性，构建贝叶斯时空模型来整合空间错位的两类数据。

Result: 通过多种情景的模拟实验评估了模型性能，并应用于波多黎各四家医院的钩端螺旋体病主动监测数据。

Conclusion: 该方法为整合空间错位的被动与主动监测数据提供了综合框架，能够更准确地估计真实疾病负担，对钩端螺旋体病等被忽视传染病尤其重要。

Abstract: Passive surveillance systems, in which data routinely collected by medical facilities are used to monitor the caseload of infectious diseases, are relatively straightforward to implement but often result in underestimation of the burden of disease due to under-diagnosis and imperfect testing. Targeted active surveillance can be used to correct these case counts to better reflect the true burden of disease. However, when the active surveillance effort is performed at a subset of hospitals and passive surveillance data is reported at an aggregated regional level, the resulting spatial misalignment must be reconciled to estimate the true rate of hospital-presenting disease at the spatial region level. Motivated by a recent active surveillance project for leptospirosis in four Puerto Rican hospitals, we address this challenge and develop a novel Bayesian spatio-temporal framework to better reflect the true number of hospital-presenting individuals with the disease. In particular, our method extends the Poisson-logistic framework to incorporate spatial heterogeneity in the probability of presenting to the hospitals across the study region. Our framework also accounts for imperfect diagnostic testing within the active surveillance data, addressing a common challenge for infectious diseases, particularly for neglected ones like leptospirosis. The model is assessed via simulation under various scenarios and then applied to the motivating leptospirosis data. Our approach offers a comprehensive framework for integrating spatially misaligned passive and active surveillance data, enabling better estimation of true disease burden.

</details>


### [91] [Estimating Distributions with Failure Rate Properties from Noisy Quantile Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13212)
*Timothy C. Y. Chan,Ningyuan Chen,Craig Fernandes,Muhammad Maaz*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对分布估计中存在的结构信息不完整和数据删失两个难题，提出了基于递增失效率（IFR）约束的极大似然估计方法。通过将无限维非凸原始问题转化为有限维凸优化问题，结合保形插值重建完整CDF，并建立了有限样本误差界和收敛速度。方法可扩展至失效率平均、新优于旧等约束，在收益管理和可靠性案例中验证了良好拟合和下游决策质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际运营场景中，分布估计常常面临两个主要困难：(1) 对分布结构缺乏完整知识，无法假设具体参数族；(2) 数据受限且存在删失（如仅知样本是否超过某个阈值）。现有方法难以同时处理这两个问题。

Method: (1) 提出IFR约束下的极大似然估计问题，识别其无限维、非凸特性；(2) 开发两步法：先求解一个有限维凸优化问题（在变换后的分位点上），再通过保形插值重建完整CDF；(3) 建立有限样本误差界与收敛速度；(4) 扩展至失效率平均、新优于旧、广义失效率等约束类型。

Result: 数值实验和收益管理、可靠性领域案例研究表明，该方法具有良好的拟合优度（goodness-of-fit），并能显著提升下游决策的优化质量。

Conclusion: 本文为处理带失效率形状约束的删失数据分布估计问题提供了一个可计算、有理论保证的框架，并为离线数据收集策略提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Estimating an unknown cumulative distribution function (cdf) from data, either as a statistical object of interest or as an input to a downstream optimization problem, is fundamental in operations. In practice, however, distribution estimation is often complicated by incomplete knowledge of the distribution's structure and limited, censored data. To address the first complication, we study distributions satisfying failure-rate shape constraints, especially increasing failure rate (IFR), rather than assuming a fully specified parametric family. To address the second, we consider noisy quantile data: at finitely many prespecified knots, each observation records only whether an independent sample lies below or above the knot. This combination arises naturally in pricing, reliability, and healthcare applications. We formulate the IFR-constrained maximum likelihood estimator and show that the original problem is infinite-dimensional and non-convex. We then develop a tractable two-step approach that solves a finite-dimensional convex optimization problem over transformed knot values and reconstructs a full cdf through shape-preserving interpolation. We establish finite-sample error bounds and convergence rates, yielding practical guidance for offline data collection. We also extend the framework to failure-rate-average, new-better-than-used, and generalized-failure-rate properties. Numerical experiments and case studies in revenue management and reliability demonstrate strong goodness-of-fit and improved downstream decision quality.

</details>


### [92] [Machine Learning-based detection of long COVID using Heart Rate Variability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13668)
*Brais Iglesias-Otero,Xosá A. Vila Sobrino,María J. Lado,Leandro Rodriguez-Liñares,Baltasar García Pérez-Schofield,Pedro Cuesta Morales,Arturo J. Méndez,María Bustillo Casado,Alexandre García-Caballero*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过机器学习分析心率变异性参数，发现Gradient Boost模型能以85.2%的准确率和0.907的AUC识别长新冠患者，证实了长新冠与HRV之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约20%的新冠感染者在痊愈后持续出现症状（长新冠），目前缺乏客观的诊断指标，亟需建立长新冠与生理参数之间的量化关联以辅助诊断。

Method: 采集每位受试者在静息、体力活动和压力状态下的5次心率记录，提取15个HRV指标（共75个特征），经特征筛选保留16个特征，训练10种ML模型（SVC、LinearSVC、LR、LDA、SGD、MLP、NB、RF、Gradient Boost、ADA Boost）。

Result: Gradient Boost表现最佳：准确率85.2%，F1-score 84.9%，AUC 0.907；所有模型AUC均超过0.833。

Conclusion: 研究表明长新冠与HRV存在关联，机器学习模型可有效识别这一关系，为长新冠的客观诊断提供辅助工具。

Abstract: After COVID epidemic has ravaged the world, around 20% of infected subjects continue to manifest symptoms several months after their cure. This disorder is called long COVID. This paper presents a study carried out at the University Hospital of Ourense with the aim of establishing a relationship among the disease and variations in Heart rate variability (HRV) parameters using machine learning (ML). Five heart rate recordings were obtained per subject, both at rest and under conditions of physical effort and stress. Each record was processed and 15 HRV indices were extracted, giving 75 features per patient. Of these features, 16 were selected to train 10 different ML models: Support Vector Classification, Linear Support Vector Classification, Logistic Regression, Linear Discriminant Analysis, Stochastic Gradient Descent, Multiple Layer Perceptron, Naive Bayes, Random Forest, and Gradient and ADA Boost Classifiers. Results show that the best model, Gradient Boost, achieves an accuracy of 85.2%, F1-score of 84.9%, and an Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve (AUC) of 0.907, and that all models exceed 0.833 AUC. This study demonstrates an association between long COVID and heart rate variability (HRV), highlighting the utility of machine learning models in identifying this relationship and supporting its diagnose.

</details>

*Jiacheng Liang,Changhan Zou*

Main category: math.AT

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的谱理论（如Zariski谱、Balmer谱、smashing框架）在可对称幺半∞-范畴中缺乏统一的理论框架，且非稳定∞-范畴中的smashing理想以及商结构的良好行为缺乏系统研究。

Method: 在可表现对称幺半∞-范畴中引入Zariski框架；将理想重新定义为指向单位对象的单态；引入Σ-平凡性和Σ-正合性两个性质来保证商结构的良好行为；构造E_∞-半环的商。

Result: Zariski框架在紧生成时是凝聚的；其谱空间同时恢复经典交换环的Zariski谱和交换2-环的Balmer谱的Hochster对偶；稳定范畴的smashing框架可等同于其对偶模范畴的Zariski框架；该理想定义在适当语境下统一了thick理想、smashing理想等概念。

Conclusion: Zariski框架为高阶代数中的谱理论提供了统一的范畴论基础，并解决了非稳定情形下的理想商结构问题。

Abstract: We introduce the Zariski frame of any presentably symmetric monoidal $\infty$-category. This allows us to unify several spectral theories arising in higher algebra. The Zariski frame is coherent whenever the category is compactly generated, and the associated spectral space recovers both the classical Zariski spectrum of a commutative ring and the Hochster dual of the Balmer spectrum of a commutative $2$-ring. Moreover, the smashing frame of any stable presentably symmetric monoidal $\infty$-category can be identified with the Zariski frame of its category of dualizable modules. This construction is based on the principle that ideals in a symmetric monoidal $\infty$-category should be understood as monomorphisms into the unit object. In suitable contexts, this notion recovers the kinds of ideals appearing in the preceding examples, including thick ideals and smashing ideals, and it also accommodates the smashing ideals of non-stable $\infty$-categories. We also study the problem of forming quotients by ideals, which is subtle in the setting of higher algebra. To address this, we introduce two properties of pointed $\infty$-categories, called $Σ$-triviality and $Σ$-exactness. These conditions ensure that quotienting by ideals behaves well. As an application, we construct quotients of $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-semirings.

</details>


### [94] [Inductive construction of path homology chains and the structure of $Ω_3(G;R)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13906)
*Matthew Burfitt,Tyrone Cutler*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过上下扩展和面多元图归纳构造有向图路径同调链模元素的方法，在特征2下生成整个链复形，在特征0下生成低维模，并用于构造显式生成元，解决了Grigor'yan提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径同调在GLMY理论中具有核心地位，但其链复形的完整描述长期以来在文献中缺失，计算路径同调需要一个两步过程，缺乏直接的归纳构造方法。

Method: 引入上下扩展（upper and lower extensions）的概念，通过面多元图（face multigraphs）参数化，建立从低维元素向高维元素归纳构造的机制。

Result: 在特征2系数下，归纳元素生成整个路径同调链复形；在特征0系数下，生成前四维（i=0,1,2,3）的同调模；归纳元素与Fu-Ivanov基在无多方图的图中一致；构造了Ω_3(G;R)的显式生成元。

Conclusion: 归纳元素为路径链复形提供了新的具体结构，可在无任何图限制的条件下直接应用于理解路径同调，并作为副产品得到了若干万有系数定理。

Abstract: Path homology plays a central role in digraph topology and GLMY theory more generally. Unfortunately, the computation of the path homology of a digraph $G$ is a two-step process, and until now no complete description of even the underlying chain complex has appeared in the literature.
  In this paper we introduce an inductive method of constructing elements of the path homology chain modules $Ω_n(G;R)$ from elements in the preceding two dimensions. This proceeds via the formation of what we call upper and lower extensions, that are parametrised by certain labelled multigraphs which we introduce and call face multigraphs.
  The inductive elements we construct generate $Ω_*(G;R)$ when $R$ has characteristic $2$. With characteristic $0$ coefficients, the inductive elements at least generate $Ω_i(G;R)$ for $i=0,1,2,3$. In low dimensions, the inductive elements coincide with the natural generators, and when the digraph contains no multisquares, the inductive elements coincide with the basis elements produced by Fu and Ivanov.
  Inductive elements provide a new concrete structure on the path chain complex that can be directly applied to understand path homology, under no restriction on the digraph $G$. We employ inductive elements to construct explicit generators of $Ω_3(G;R)$ for a ring $R$ of characteristic $0$ or $2$, answering an open question posed by Grigor'yan. Several universal coefficient statements for path homology are obtained as a byproduct.

</details>


### [95] [Antipodal paths in covers of spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13964)
*David Ellis,Maria-Romina Ivan,Imre Leader,John M. Mackay*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了当球面S^n被k个开集覆盖且n≥2k-2时，必有一个开集包含一条端点对径的路径，且该结果是最优的。这可视作Norine关于超立方体边染色猜想的球面类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究球面覆盖与对径点路径存在性的关系，并建立与Norine猜想的联系。

Method: 通过拓扑组合方法，分析球面被开集覆盖时的对径路径存在性条件，并证明n≥2k-2时的充分性及n<2k-2时的反例构造。

Result: 证明了当n≥2k-2时球面覆盖中必存在包含对径端点路径的开集，且该边界条件是最优的。

Conclusion: 给出了球面覆盖的一个组合几何性质，并为Norine猜想提供了球面版本的类比支持。

Abstract: In this note we show that if the sphere $\mathbb{S}^n$ is covered by $k$ open sets with $n \geq 2k-2$, then one of these sets contains a path with antipodal endpoints. This is best possible in the sense that the statement fails for $n < 2k-2$. The result can be seen as a spherical analogue of a well-known conjecture of Norine on edge-colourings of the discrete hypercube.

</details>

### [96] [When is the combined load identifiable from a stress-intensity profile? A coupled forward-inverse study on SIFBench finite-element data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13074)
*Giansalvo Cirrincione,Filippo Grassia*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究从应力强度因子剖面反演裂纹所受张力、弯曲和承载载荷相对大小的问题。核心贡献不在于现场载荷恢复，而在于严格刻画载荷可辨识性条件，并给出在不可辨识时能返回校准不确定性的估计器。通过SIFBench有限元数据验证，角裂纹场景中大部分几何构型适定，少数本质上病态，点估计在前者可靠，在后者无信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有裂纹载荷反演方法缺乏对可辨识性的严格理论分析，无法判断何时载荷恢复结果是可靠的、何时因载荷组合几乎等价而不可区分。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，建立载荷可辨识性的几何判据，并提供能量化不确定性的反演框架。

Method: 利用已知几何下从载荷到应力强度因子剖面的正演映射是线性的特性，将可辨识性归结为三个基本载荷剖面沿裂纹前沿的线性无关性。引入裂纹前沿算子作为结构化正演代理和可微映射，构建单纯形约束的集值反演估计器。通过稳定性裕度而非条件数来刻画病态程度。

Result: 在SIFBench角裂纹场景中，经验结果与理论一致：大多数几何构型适定，但相当一部分本质上病态；点估计在适定情形可靠，在病态情形无信息。通过受控合成噪声完成验证。

Conclusion: 载荷可辨识性由三个基本载荷剖面函数的线性相关性决定，稳定性裕度控制病态程度；所提框架能在适定和病态情形下分别提供可靠点估计和校准的不确定性量。

Abstract: This work studies the inverse problem of recovering the relative magnitudes of the tension, bending, and bearing loads acting on a crack from its stress-intensity-factor profile along the crack front, using the public SIFBench finite-element data. The central claim is not forensic load recovery on field cases, but a rigorous characterization of when the combined load is identifiable at all, together with an estimator that returns calibrated uncertainty precisely in the regimes where it is not. For a known geometry the forward map from loads to profile is exactly linear, and identifiability reduces to a single geometric question: whether the three elementary load profiles are linearly independent as functions along the front. When they are nearly dependent, many different load combinations produce almost the same profile and the inverse problem is illposed; the analysis shows that the degree of ill-posedness is controlled by an intrinsic stability margin, not by the conditioning number alone. A single crack-front operator serves both as a structured forward surrogate and as the differentiable map required by a simplex-constrained, set-valued inverse estimator. On the SIFBench corner-crack scenario the empirical behaviour matches the theory: the typical geometry is well posed while a sizable minority is genuinely ill-posed, so a point estimate is reliable on the majority and provably uninformative on the rest. Validation is on controlled synthetic noise; no real fracture cases are used or claimed.

</details>


### [97] [Tensor-Network Finite Elements for Analytic Operator Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13129)
*Abhijatmedhi Chotrattanapituk,Michael J. Landry,Chu-Liang Fu,Mingda Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个连接有限元方法（FE）与张量网络（TN）的框架，用于解析算子方程。通过FE离散化诱导的多线性相互作用张量层次结构，将高度非线性的偏微分方程转化为线性矩阵方程，并以加权残差优化问题形式在TN自由度上重新表述。一维线性和非线性扩散的矩阵乘积态计算在控制误差的同时再现了传统解，为基于TN和量子启发的算子方程求解提供了通用变分语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有限元方法将连续算子方程离散为有限维代数系统，而张量网络为关联离散系统提供灵活的变分表示，但二者之间缺乏统一的连接框架。作者旨在建立FE与TN之间的形式化联系，为非线性算子方程提供统一的变分语言和高效的求解方法。

Method: 通过FE离散化诱导多线性相互作用张量的层次结构，将微分、积分、非线性、记忆和延迟方程统一纳入共同的代数结构；将所得系统重新表述为张量网络自由度上的加权残差优化问题；采用矩阵乘积态（MPS）对一维线性和非线性扩散方程进行求解。

Result: 矩阵乘积态计算在一维线性和非线性扩散问题中，在可控制的误差范围内再现了传统有限元解，同时保持了连续性和诺伊曼边界条件。

Conclusion: 该框架为解析算子方程提供了通用的变分语言，在FE数值形式主义和TN变分算法之间建立了直接联系，为基于TN和量子启发的算子方程求解方法奠定了通用基础。

Abstract: Operator equations (OEs) underpin quantitative modeling across science and engineering. Finite-element (FE) methods discretize continuous OEs into finite-dimensional algebraic systems, whereas tensor networks (TNs) provide flexible variational representations of correlated discrete systems. Here, we develop a framework that connects FE with TN for analytic OEs. The power of this method comes from its ability to convert highly non-linear partial differential equations into linear matrix equations. In particular, we show that FE discretization induces a hierarchy of multilinear interaction tensors, through which differential, integral, nonlinear, memory, and delay equations can be expressed within a common algebraic structure. The resulting systems are reformulated as weighted-residual optimization problems over TN degrees of freedom. Matrix-product-state calculations for one-dimensional linear and nonlinear diffusion reproduce conventional solutions with controlled error while preserving continuity and Neumann boundary conditions. The framework provides a common variational language for analytic OEs and establishes a direct connection between FE numerical formalism and TN variational algorithms, offering a general foundation for TN-based and quantum-inspired approaches to solving OEs.

</details>


### [98] [A Generative Model-Free Form Deformation Approach for the Generation of Mesh Motions with Applications to PDE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13202)
*Gugliemo Padula,Artem Sinitsa,Gianluigi Rozza*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种拓扑无关的3D形状形变匹配框架，通过ODE建模形变，用自由形变(FFD)参数化速度场，并证明ODE映射是亏格0曲面间映射的通用近似器。结合TarFlow的流式生成方法学习紧凑的潜在参数化，支持高效采样和优化，在形变体流动基准上展示了改进的精度和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D形状形变匹配方法通常受限于网格拓扑的同构要求，且缺乏理论表达性保证。需要一种拓扑无关、具有理论保证且支持高效降阶建模的形变匹配框架。

Method: 1) 将形变建模为ODE流，速度场由时间相关的自由形变(FFD)参数化，通过粗控制格点的位移表达，实现形变模型与源/目标表面离散化的解耦。2) 在温和正则性假设下证明ODE映射是亏格0曲面间映射的通用近似器。3) 将ODE-FFD模型与TarFlow框架的流式生成方法结合，学习FFD映射时间序列的紧凑潜在参数化，支持概率推断和高效采样。

Result: 在形变体流动基准上，所构建的降阶模型比现有方法具有更高的精度和计算效率。

Conclusion: 提出的拓扑无关形变匹配框架兼具理论表达性保证和实际计算效率，为3D形状形变的降阶建模提供了可扩展的有效方案。

Abstract: We introduce a topology-agnostic framework for matching deformations of three-dimensional shapes with non-isomorphic mesh graphs by modelling the deformation as the flow of an Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE). The velocity field is parameterised by a time-dependent Free Form Deformation (FFD), expressed through displacements of a coarse control lattice, yielding a smooth and low-dimensional representation that decouples the deformation model from the discretisation of the source and target surfaces. Under mild regularity assumptions, we prove that the induced ODE map is a universal approximator (in the sup norm) for mappings between genus-0 surfaces, providing a theoretical expressivity guarantee. To further compress the representation and enable probabilistic inference, we couple the ODE--FFD model with a flow-based generative approach in the TarFlow framework, learning a compact latent parametrisation over time series of FFD maps. The resulting method supports efficient sampling and optimisation of plausible deformation trajectories while preserving mesh quality, and it enables scalable reduced-order modelling. Experiments on deforming-body flow benchmarks demonstrate improved accuracy and computational efficiency of reduced-order models constructed from the learned latent dynamics.

</details>


### [99] [Gaussian FSBP operators: Comparison and application to numerical methods for hyperbolic conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13224)
*Jan Glaubitz,Henry Haase,Philipp Öffner,Finnja Stafforst*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于广义高斯求积的开放和闭合FSBP算子，并扩展了函数空间精确外推算子以支持开放节点分布，通过一维线性平流、无粘Burgers和可压缩Euler方程实验证明其在时变问题中的效率和精度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FSBP算子多基于等距网格，广义高斯求积虽能减少网格点数量，但开放节点分布下的FSBP框架尚不完善，且高斯FSBP算子在更具挑战性的时变问题中的优势尚未充分验证。

Method: 比较基于广义高斯求积构造的开放与闭合FSBP算子；引入函数空间精确外推算子以支持开放节点分布；应用于一维线性平流、无粘Burgers和可压缩Euler方程进行数值求解。

Result: FSBP算子在数值格式中能够提高效率和精度，在更具挑战性的时变设定下相比近期高斯FSBP相关工作展现出优势。

Conclusion: 广义高斯求积结合开放/闭合FSBP算子及外推扩展，可有效提升非多项式逼近空间下双曲守恒律数值方法的性能。

Abstract: Function-space summation-by-parts (FSBP) operators enable conservative and energy-stable numerical methods for hyperbolic conservation laws based on general, non-polynomial approximation spaces. Recent works show that using generalized Gaussian quadrature significantly reduces the number of grid points required compared to existing constructions that have mostly focused on equidistant grids. In this paper, we compare open and closed FSBP operators constructed with generalized Gaussian quadratures and apply them to numerically solve hyperbolic conservation laws. Furthermore, to support open node distributions, we extend the FSBP framework by introducing function-space exact extrapolation operators and operationalize them in numerical schemes for solving hyperbolic conservation laws. Our numerical experiments include the one-dimensional linear advection, non-viscous Burgers, and compressible Euler equations of gas dynamics. We observe that applying FSBP operators in numerical schemes can improve efficiency and accuracy. Notably, we demonstrate these advantages in more challenging time-dependent settings compared to other recent works on Gaussian FSBP operators.

</details>


### [100] [A linear, fully decoupled, and unconditionally energy-stable SAV-FEM for the Cahn--Hilliard--Navier--Stokes model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13253)
*Jinting Yang,Nianyu Yi,Peimeng Yin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对Cahn-Hilliard-Navier-Stokes系统，提出一种基于SAV方法的线性、完全解耦、无条件能量稳定的有限元格式，仅引入单个标量辅助变量及新型更新策略，实现了所有非线性项的线性化解耦处理，并证明了离散能量耗散律、稳定性和最优阶L²误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAV方法处理CHNS系统时通常需要引入多个辅助变量或额外技术来处理不同类型的非线性项，数值实现复杂。本文旨在开发一种更简洁、高效的格式，仅使用单个辅助变量即可完成系统重构，以简化计算并保持能量稳定性。

Method: 引入单个标量辅助变量及新型更新策略对CHNS系统中所有非线性项进行重新表述；时间上采用IMEX Euler格式（线性项隐式、非线性项显式处理）；空间上采用有限元离散；最终全离散格式分解为两个线性子问题和一个标量二次代数方程。

Result: 证明了无条件离散能量耗散律及多个相关范数下的稳定性；推导了全离散有限元逼近的最优阶L²误差估计；数值实验验证了理论结果和算法效率。

Conclusion: 该格式具有线性、完全解耦、无条件能量稳定的优良特性，且实现简单高效，为CHNS系统提供了一种有效的数值求解方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a linear, fully decoupled, and unconditionally energy-stable fully discrete finite element scheme for the Cahn--Hilliard--Navier--Stokes (CHNS) system by employing the scalar auxiliary variable (SAV) approach. Unlike existing SAV-based formulations that typically introduce multiple auxiliary variables or additional techniques to handle different nonlinearities, we introduce only one scalar auxiliary variable together with a novel update of the auxiliary variable to reformulate all nonlinear terms arising from the Cahn--Hilliard and Navier--Stokes equations, yielding an equivalent reformulation of the original CHNS system. An implicit--explicit (IMEX) Euler scheme is applied for temporal discretization, where the linear terms are treated implicitly and the nonlinear terms explicitly, while a finite element method is adopted for spatial discretization. The resulting fully discrete scheme can be efficiently decomposed into two linear subproblems and one scalar quadratic algebraic equation, which significantly simplifies the implementation. Furthermore, we prove that the proposed scheme satisfies an unconditional discrete energy dissipation law and establish its stability with respect to several relevant norms. Optimal-order $L^2$ error estimates are also derived for the fully discrete finite element approximation. Finally, a series of numerical experiments are presented to verify the theoretical results and demonstrate the efficiency of the proposed method.

</details>


### [101] [Duality Framework for Flux Constrained Flow in Porous Media: Analysis and Numerics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13256)
*Harbir Antil,Keegan L. A. Kirk,Felipe Pérez*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究饱和多孔介质中受双侧通量约束的Darcy流动问题，建立速度-对偶能量最大化形式，通过Fenchel对偶得到压力原对偶公式与强对偶性，推导出无经验常数的后验误差恒等式，并给出了Raviart–Thomas/Crouzeix–Raviart离散格式的误差分析与自适应算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对饱和多孔介质中边界法向通量受双边不等式约束的Darcy流动问题，缺乏系统的变分框架、对偶理论及可靠的数值分析方法。

Method: 1. 将问题建模为H(div;Ω)凸子集上速度对偶凹泛函的最大化问题；2. 利用Fenchel对偶导出基于压力的预对偶形式，建立强对偶性与互补性最优条件；3. 推导无经验常数的原-对偶间隙后验误差恒等式；4. 采用Raviart–Thomas/Crouzeix–Raviart离散格式，给出离散误差恒等式与先验误差收敛率；5. 提出基于局部原-对偶间隙指标的自适应细化方法。

Result: 建立了完整的对偶变分框架与强对偶定理；获得了无任意常数的后验误差恒等式；推导了离散格式在分数阶正则性假设下的先验误差收敛率；数值实验验证了理论结果与自适应算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的对偶变分方法与离散格式能有效处理带边界通量约束的Darcy流动问题，原-对偶间隙可作为可靠的误差指示器驱动自适应网格细化。

Abstract: We introduce and analyze Darcy flow through a saturated porous medium subject to bilateral constraints on the normal flux across a portion of the boundary. The problem is posed as the maximization of a velocity-based dual concave energy over a convex subset of $H(\mathrm{div};Ω)$; Fenchel duality identifies a pressure-based predual formulation, yields strong duality, and provides convex optimality conditions with a complementarity structure on the constrained boundary. The primal--dual gap satisfies an a posteriori error identity, free of generic constants, valid for arbitrary admissible approximations. The duality structure is inherited by a Raviart--Thomas/Crouzeix--Raviart discretization, from which we derive a discrete error identity and a priori error decay rates under fractional regularity assumptions on the solution and the flux bounds. Numerical experiments, including adaptive refinement driven by localized primal--dual gap indicators, support the theory.

</details>


### [102] [A Weighted Integral-Regularized Finite Difference Scheme for the Tempered Fractional Laplacian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13317)
*Mingyi Wang,Lisen Ding,Dongling Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对 tempered 分数阶拉普拉斯算子（TFL）的本征奇异性问题，提出了一种加权积分正则化有限差分（WIRFD）方法，通过多维泰勒展开与平滑窗函数正则化奇异核，实现了 O(h^{4-α}) 的截断误差界，并在一维情形下证明了 l^2 和 l^∞ 范数的稳定性与最优收敛性，结合 FFT 加速的 PCG 方法高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TFL 算子的本征奇异性导致现有数值方法难以同时兼顾精度、效率和实现简便性，亟需一种能有效处理该奇异性的数值格式。

Method: 提出加权积分正则化有限差分（WIRFD）方法：利用多维泰勒展开结合平滑窗函数正则化奇异被积函数；将积分分解为规则化项（用 punctured 梯形法则离散）和可直接计算的校正项。对多维 TFL 算子，引入光滑辅助函数与混叠公式导出 O(h^{4-α}) 截断误差界。离散矩阵的 Toeplitz 结构支持 FFT 加速矩阵-向量乘法，采用预处理共轭梯度（PCG）法求解线性系统。

Result: 对 α∈(0,2) 且 u∈C^s(R^d) (s≥8) 的多维 TFL 算子，推导出 l^∞ 范数下 O(h^{4-α}) 截断误差界；对一维 TFL 方程，基于离散矩阵的严格对角占优性和最小特征值下界，建立了 l^2 和 l^∞ 范数的稳定性及 α∈[1,2) 时 O(h^{4-α}) 最优收敛性。数值实验验证了方法的精度、效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: WIRFD 方法有效克服了 TFL 算子的奇异性难题，在精度（四阶）、稳定性和计算效率（FFT+PCG）方面均表现优异，为分数阶拉普拉斯算子的数值逼近提供了一种可靠且易于实现的新框架。

Abstract: The intrinsic singularity of the tempered fractional Laplacian (TFL) remains a major challenge in developing numerical methods that are simultaneously accurate, efficient, and easy to implement. We develop a weighted integral-regularized finite difference (WIRFD) method that regularizes the singular integrand via a multidimensional Taylor expansion incorporating a smooth window function. The resulting integral is decomposed into a regularized term, which is discretized by a punctured trapezoidal rule, and a directly evaluated correction term. For the multidimensional TFL operator, we derive an $O(h^{4-α})$ truncation error bound in the $l^{\infty}$-norm for $α\in(0,2)$ and $u\in C^s(\mathbb{R}^d)$ with $s\geq 8$ by introducing a smooth auxiliary function together with the aliasing formula. For the one-dimensional TFL equation, we establish stability in both the $l^2$- and $l^{\infty}$-norms and optimal $O(h^{4-α})$ convergence for $α\in[1,2)$ based on the strict diagonal dominance of the discrete matrix and a lower bound for its minimum eigenvalue. The Toeplitz structure of the discrete matrix enables FFT-based matrix-vector multiplication, and the resulting linear systems are solved efficiently by a preconditioned conjugate gradient (PCG) method. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical results, demonstrating the accuracy, efficiency, and robustness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [103] [Data driven non-equilibrium moist phase exchanges for atmospheric convection within a discontinuous Galerkin model of the compressible Euler equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13360)
*David Lee,Kieran Ricardo,Junwei Lyu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文训练神经网络学习大气对流中水汽、液态水和冰相间的质量交换，在热力学平衡和机械非平衡两种假设下制定损失函数，并将该网络以热力学一致的方式集成到2D非静力大气模型中，用于模拟亚公里分辨率下的三相云形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于物理的三相湿过程参数化计算成本高且依赖热力学平衡假设，本文旨在利用神经网络高效学习相变过程，同时保持热力学一致性，以提升亚公里分辨率对流模拟的精度和效率。

Method: 训练数据来自LFRic区域模式（含CASIM多矩微物理参数化）的对流解析输出；损失函数基于热力学平衡（各相温度压力相同）和机械非平衡（各相吉布斯自由能不同）两种假设制定；网络输出保证质量守恒，熵变由网络输出推导以保持能量守恒；将训练好的神经网络嵌入2D垂直切片间断伽辽金非静力湿大气模型中。

Result: 成功模拟了三相云形成过程，并与基于热力学平衡的物理方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: 神经网络方法能够以热力学一致的方式学习并替代传统三相微物理参数化，在亚公里分辨率对流模拟中具有潜力。

Abstract: A neural network is trained to learn the mass exchanges between vapour, liquid and ice phases in atmospheric convection. The network is trained on convection resolving output from a regional configuration of the LFRic model with a multi-moment microphysics parameterisation (CASIM). The loss function for learning these phase exchanges is formulated under the assumptions of thermal and mechanical equilibrium (same temperature and pressure for all phases), and mechanical dis-equilibrium (different Gibbs free energies for all phases). The network outputs determine the exchanges of vapour, liquid and ice so as to conserve mass, and the resulting change in entropy is determined from the network outputs so as to conserve energy. The neural network is implemented in a thermodynamically consistent manner within a 2D vertical slice discontinuous Galerkin model of a moist, non-hydrostatic atmosphere in order to simulate the formation of three-phase clouds for convection at sub-km resolution. The results are compared to those from a physics based representation of three-phase moist processes at thermodynamic equilibrium.

</details>


### [104] [Hybrid GKB Methods for X-Ray Tomography Problems with Unmatched Back Projector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13381)
*Abdulmajeed Alsubhi,Rosemary Renaut*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出针对CT重建中不匹配投影算子对（forward/back projection）的混合AB和BA Golub-Kahan双对角化（GKB）方法，通过在投影过程中引入正则化，利用投影空间的SVD高效求解并自动选择正则化参数（L-curve或GCV），有效抑制半收敛效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT重建中，前向投影A和反向投影B常因计算效率采用不同离散化方案导致算子不匹配（非精确伴随关系），这种不匹配改变了重建问题的数学性质，影响迭代重建方法的收敛行为。现有AB/BA-GKB及GMRES方法虽能减轻半收敛效应，但缺乏有效的正则化机制。

Method: 提出混合AB-GKB和混合BA-GKB算法：在GKB双对角化过程中引入正则化；利用投影空间上的奇异值分解（SVD）高效重建解；采用L-curve或广义交叉验证（GCV）自动选择正则化参数。

Result: 在多个CT重建问题的数值实验中，所提出的混合方法在抑制半收敛效应方面表现出有效性，相比传统方法具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 混合AB/BA-GKB方法通过内嵌正则化与自动参数选择，有效解决了不匹配投影算子CT重建中的半收敛问题，提升了迭代重建的稳定性和实用性。

Abstract: X ray Computed Tomography (CT) is a widely used imaging modality in medical, industrial, and scientific applications. In practical CT implementations, forward, A, and back projection, B, operators are often constructed using different discretization schemes to improve computational efficiency on available software and hardware platforms. Consequently, the resulting projector pair is generally unmatched, meaning that the back projector is not the exact adjoint of the forward projector. This mismatch alters the mathematical properties of the reconstruction problem and can affect the convergence behavior of iterative reconstruction methods. Previous studies have proposed AB and BA Golub Kahan bidiagonalization (GKB) methods, as well as GMRES and hybrid GMRES methods, for solving CT reconstruction problems with unmatched projector pairs, demonstrating reduced semiconvergence effects compared with conventional approaches. In this work, we develop hybrid variants of the AB- and BA-GKB algorithms by incorporating regularization within the projection process. The singular value decomposition for the operator on the projected space is used to efficiently reconstruct the solution, and to automatically select the regularization parameter using either the L-curve or generalized cross-validation. Numerical experiments on several CT reconstruction problems demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed hybrid methods in improving robustness against semiconvergence.

</details>


### [105] [Residual-Christoffel Sampling for Random Feature Collocation of Linear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13382)
*Jiale Linghu,Yangshuai Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种算子感知的随机特征配点法，通过残差-Christoffel密度和逆密度白化映射构造稳定离散格式，理论证明白化后的内积Gram矩阵是参考Gram的谱近似，样本复杂度与保留维度成线性关系（对数因子内），实验表明该方法在多种方程和几何中显著降低条件数和迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机特征配点法的稳定性取决于采样残差方程是否能够表示微分算子所诱导的几何结构，现有方法缺乏对这种几何结构的显式建模，导致离散系统可能病态。

Method: 提出算子感知离散化：1) 算子作用后的特征同时决定配点测度（残差-Christoffel密度）和系数白化映射（逆密度权重）；2) 确定性标量行替代方案通过最大化正则化对数行列式增量逐次选取；3) 理论分析白化后Gram矩阵的谱逼近性质。

Result: 1) 白化内积Gram是参考Gram的谱近似，样本复杂度O(r log r)（r为保留维度）；2) 均匀解析残差核的特征值呈 stretched-exponential 衰减，岭有效维度为多对数尺度；3) 实验在标量/向量方程、1-3维几何上得到数值满秩变换系统，条件数和迭代次数大幅降低。

Conclusion: 残差空间几何为稳定强形式随机特征配点提供了理论上有原则的设计框架，确定性构造在最小标量样本量下取得最低误差。

Abstract: Random feature collocation fixes a randomly generated trial space and determines its coefficients from a linear least-squares system. Stability then depends on whether the sampled residual equations represent the geometry induced by the differential operator. We construct an operator-aware discretization in which the operator-applied features determine both the collocation measure and a coefficient whitening map. The randomized scheme combines a residual-Christoffel density with inverse-density weights, while a deterministic scalar-row alternative maximizes successive regularized log-determinant increments. Conditional on the realized trial space, the sampled whitened interior Gram is a spectral approximation to the reference Gram on the retained residual space, with sample complexity linear in the retained dimension up to a logarithmic factor. For uniformly analytic residual kernels, the associated operator has stretched-exponentially decaying eigenvalues and ridge effective dimension that is polylogarithmic in the inverse ridge scale. Experiments on scalar and vector equations, varied geometries, and one to three spatial dimensions show that residual-space sampling and whitening produce numerically full-rank transformed systems with substantially smaller condition numbers and iteration counts. The deterministic construction attains the lowest errors at the smallest scalar sample sizes. Residual-space geometry therefore yields a principled design for stable strong-form random feature collocation.

</details>


### [106] [Inverse scattering for three-dimensional random obstacles with multi-frequency data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13473)
*Zhiqi Sun,Yiwen Lin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对三维光滑星形障碍物在随机各向同性波动下的逆声散射问题，提出了一种基于蒙特卡洛的多频递归线性化算法，用于同时恢复障碍物几何形状和形状不确定性统计信息（包括参考几何、KL特征值、协方差超参数及边际分布），并证明了远场数据概率律对径向函数分布的唯一确定性，数值实验验证了该方法在高斯和非高斯随机变化下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际散射体形状常因物理或环境因素存在不确定性几何变化，而逆散射问题本身是病态的，这类几何不确定性会对恢复过程产生不可忽略的影响。现有方法多假设散射体几何确定，缺乏对形状不确定性及其统计信息的联合恢复研究。

Method: 提出蒙特卡洛多频递归线性化算法：将远场算子对几何参数进行线性化，利用频率延拓技术从粗到细恢复未知几何；基于重建样本进一步估计参考几何、KL特征值、协方差超参数（高斯扰动）及代表性边际分布（非高斯扰动）。同时证明了远场数据概率律唯一确定径向函数分布的理论结论。

Result: 理论证明远场数据概率律唯一确定径向函数的分布，进而唯一确定参考形状及相关统计量；数值实验表明该方法在高斯和非高斯随机扰动下均能有效恢复散射体形状及统计信息。

Conclusion: 该方法能够同时恢复障碍物几何形状和形状不确定性的关键统计信息，为存在几何不确定性的逆散射问题提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In many practical scenarios the shapes of scatterers exhibit uncertain geometric variations arising from diverse physical or environmental factors. For inverse scattering problems which are inherently ill-posed, the presence of such geometric uncertainties may have a non-negligible impact on the recovery process. With the aim of recovering both obstacle geometry and statistics of the shape uncertainties, in this paper we study an inverse acoustic scattering problem for three-dimensional smooth star-shaped obstacles with random isotropic fluctuations. We propose an efficient Monte Carlo-based multi-frequency recursive linearization algorithm in which the far-field operator is linearized with respect to the geometry parameters and frequency continuation is employed to recover the unknown geometry from coarse to fine scales. Based on the reconstructed samples, we further estimate the reference geometry and key statistics of the shape fluctuation field including Karhunen--Loève eigenvalues, covariance hyper-parameters for Gaussian perturbations and covariance structure, representative marginal distributions for non-Gaussian perturbations. We also prove that the probability law of the far-field data uniquely determines the radial function in distribution which implies uniqueness of the reference shape and related statistics. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in recovering both the scatterer shapes and the associated statistical information under Gaussian and non-Gaussian random variations.

</details>


### [107] [Reconstruct the ambient noise source from the multi-frequency sparse correlation data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13509)
*Hao Gu,Hongxia Guo,Xiang Xu,Yue Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的多频因子分解方法，用于重建环境噪声源的空间支撑集。该方法仅需要稀疏的相关数据且计算成本低，通过二维和三维数值实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境噪声源空间支撑集的重建在多个领域具有重要应用，但现有方法通常需要密集的数据采样或较高的计算成本。作者旨在开发一种更高效、数据需求更低的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种多频因子分解方法（multi-frequency factorization method），利用稀疏的相关数据（sparse correlation data）来重建噪声源的空间支撑集，算法计算复杂度较低。

Result: 在二维和三维数值实验中，该方法成功重建了环境噪声源的空间支撑集，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该多频因子分解方法能以低计算成本和稀疏数据有效重建环境噪声源的空间支撑集，为相关应用提供了一种实用的技术方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a novel multi-frequency factorization method to reconstruct the spatial support of the ambient noise source. The proposed method only requires sparse correlation data and has low computational cost. Numerical experiments in two and three dimensions are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [108] [Computing Strong Rank-Revealing Factorizations for Matrices with Orthonormal Rows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13532)
*Anil Damle*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Stewart枢轴策略的强秩揭示分解方法，适用于行正交矩阵。与GKS列选择算法结合后，能实现与直接应用强秩揭示分解相同的秩-k近似精度和基条件数。研究进一步扩展到两个方向：(1)分析仅使用右奇异向量近似时的GKS算法；(2)提出随机化变体，在保持相同理论保证的同时，比确定性方法快两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强秩揭示分解在应用于一般矩阵时计算代价较高，且GKS列选择算法在仅有近似奇异向量信息时缺乏理论分析。需要更高效且保持理论保证的列选择与秩揭示方法。

Method: 采用Stewart枢轴策略（基于Bischof工作）处理行正交矩阵；将其与GKS算法结合以获得近似精度和基条件数保证；进一步提出随机化变体以加速计算。

Result: 该组合方法达到了与直接应用强秩揭示分解相同的秩-k近似精度和基条件数界限；随机化变体在保持相同理论保证下实现了两个数量级的加速。

Conclusion: Stewart枢轴策略可有效用于行正交矩阵的强秩揭示分解，与GKS结合效果优良，随机化扩展在效率上具有显著优势。

Abstract: We show that a pivoting strategy due to Stewart (based on work by Bischof) computes a strong rank-revealing factorization when applied to a matrix with orthonormal rows. When paired with the classical column selection algorithm of Golub, Klema, and Stewart (GKS) it helps achieve rank-$k$ approximation accuracy bounds and basis conditioning as good as those from applying a strong rank-revealing factorization directly to A. We then extend this framework in two directions: (1) providing analysis of GKS when only approximations of right singular vectors are available and (2) providing a randomized variant of the pivoting strategy for matrices with orthonormal rows that achieves the same theoretical guarantees but can return the desired subset two orders of magnitude faster than the deterministic variant.

</details>


### [109] [A Spectrally Damped Tensor Randomized Kaczmarz Method for Doubly Noisy Tensor Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13552)
*Nahyun Lee,Jiyoung Choi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了张量随机Kaczmarz方法在双噪声扰动模型下的行为，并提出了一种谱阻尼方法（SD-TRK），通过频域依赖的阻尼参数实现稳定收敛，理论推导了误差递归与速度-鲁棒性权衡，实验验证了其在噪声和病态条件下的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中张量线性系统常面临系统张量和右端项同时包含噪声的双重扰动，标准TRK方法在此类不一致系统下会出现噪声限制和半收敛行为，需要理论分析和改进算法。

Method: 提出谱阻尼张量随机Kaczmarz方法（SD-TRK），基于FFT实现频域逐切片阻尼更新；推导了标准TRK和SD-TRK的期望误差递归，分离了误差传播与噪声注入项，并证明了速度-鲁棒性权衡关系。

Result: 标准TRK的误差递归包含一个收缩项和一个持续扰动项，解释了噪声限制和半收敛行为；SD-TRK的误差递归显式分离了误差传播与噪声注入，给出了速度-鲁棒性权衡的显式边界。合成张量系统和图像重建实验验证了SD-TRK在噪声和病态条件下的稳定表现。

Conclusion: SD-TRK通过频域依赖的阻尼参数有效缓解了双噪声扰动下的不一致性问题，在噪声和病态条件下相比标准TRK具有更稳定的收敛行为，但存在速度与鲁棒性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Tensor randomized Kaczmarz (TRK) methods are efficient row-action solvers for tensor linear systems under the t-product framework. We study their behavior under a doubly noisy perturbation model. In this model, both the system tensor and the right-hand side tensor are corrupted. We first analyze standard TRK and derive an expected error recursion with two terms. One term is contractive, and the other is a persistent perturbation term. This explains the noise-limited and semi-convergent behavior that can occur when the observed tensor system is inconsistent. We then introduce a spectrally damped tensor randomized Kaczmarz method (SD-TRK). We prove an expected error recursion for SD-TRK that separates error propagation from noise injection. The bound makes explicit a speed-robustness trade-off. We also give an FFT-based implementation that applies the damped update slice-wise in the Fourier domain. This implementation allows frequency-dependent damping parameters in practice. Numerical experiments on synthetic tensor systems illustrate the stabilization behavior of SD-TRK relative to standard TRK in noisy and ill-conditioned settings. We also include a two-pass image reconstruction comparison under the same noisy reconstruction pipeline.

</details>


### [110] [Spectral-Informed Neural Networks Outperform Spectral Methods in High-dimensional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13566)
*Tianchi Yu,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出Modified SINNs，通过集成系数衰减缩放和基嵌入来提升高维问题上谱方法与PINN结合的准确率，实验证明在中高维问题上优于稀疏网格谱方法和PINNs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱方法在低维问题中精度极高，但面临维度灾难；PINNs可扩展至高维但精度和效率有限。现有SINNs虽结合二者优势，仍需进一步提升高维问题的准确性。

Method: 在SINNs基础上引入系数衰减缩放（coefficient decay scaling）和基于调和分析的基嵌入（basis embeddings），直接在谱域操作以避免空间导数计算并降低内存消耗。

Result: 数值实验表明：Modified SINNs在含不完全谱信息的中维问题上优于稀疏网格谱方法；在高维问题上相比PINNs实现了更优的准确性。

Conclusion: Modified SINNs有效缓解了谱方法的维度灾难，同时提升了PINNs在高维问题上的精度，是一种有前景的高维PDE求解方法。

Abstract: For low-dimensional problems ($d\leq3$), spectral methods can achieve exceptionally high accuracy. For middle-dimensional problems ($4 \leq d \lesssim 10$), spectral methods remain feasible through specific techniques such as sparse grids or hyperbolic cross. However, for high-dimensional problems ($d\gg 10$), spectral methods suffer frome the curse of dimensionality. Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a promising approach to overcome this challenge, offering scalability to high dimensions, but often suffer from limited accuracy and efficiency. Recently proposed spectral-informed neural networks (SINNs) combine spectral methods with PINNs, operating directly in the spectral domain to avoid spatial derivative computations and to reduce memory consumption. In this work, we introduce Modified SINNs, which integrate coefficient decay scaling and basis embeddings motivated by harmonic analysis to enhance accuracy in high-dimensional problems and enable accurate approximation of unknown spectral coefficients. Numerical experiments on steady and time-dependent partial differential equations demonstrate that Modified SINNs outperform sparse grid spectral methods on middle-dimensional problems with incomplete spectral information and achieve superior accuracy compared to PINNs on high-dimensional problems.

</details>


### [111] [Approximation of solutions of parameter-dependent problems by residual neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13574)
*Ana Carpio*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于梯度流的收敛性训练方案，用于训练含解析激活函数的神经网络，通过Lojasiewicz理论保证收敛性，并将网络系数近似为常微分方程组的解，在参数化问题和反问题中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种实现简单且具有收敛性保证的神经网络训练方法，克服传统训练方法缺乏理论收敛保证的问题。

Method: 基于梯度流（gradient flows）构建训练方案，利用Lojasiewicz理论证明收敛性，将神经网络系数近似为求解常微分方程组的问题。

Result: 在残差神经网络近似参数化问题的解时，能够正确复现简单ODE对少数参数的依赖性；在涉及波动约束的逆问题中，即使问题严重不适定，也能合理近似。

Conclusion: 该方法实现简单、有理论收敛保证，能有效处理参数化前向问题和不适定逆问题。

Abstract: We develop a convergent scheme to train neural networks involving analytic activation functions based on gradient flows. Convergence properties are guaranteed by Lojasiewicz theory. The main advantage of this approach is its simplicity of implementation. The coefficients of the network are approximated by solving a system of ordinary differential equations. We test the method by constructing residual neural network approximations of solutions of parametric problems. The dependence of the solutions of simple ordinary differential equations on a few parameters is correctly reproduced. The solutions of inverse problems involving wave constraints which depend on a few parameters can be reasonably approximated, even in regions in which the problem is severely ill posed.

</details>


### [112] [Adaptive space-time BEM for the heat equation with Neumann boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13578)
*Gregor Gantner,Helmut Harbrecht,Franz Nowakowsky*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有给定初始和Neumann条件的热方程，提出了时空边界元法（BEM）的加权残差后验误差估计器，该估计器是未知BEM误差的上界。在空间二维域上的数值实验中，由该估计器驱动的自适应算法相比均匀网格细化显著加快了收敛速度，即使在存在强奇异性的情况下也能达到接近最优的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了高效求解具有初始和Neumann边界条件的热方程，需要能够指导自适应网格细化的可靠后验误差估计器，以在存在奇异性的情况下实现最优收敛速率。

Method: 提出加权残差后验误差估计器作为BEM误差的上界；采用抛物尺度缩放的三棱柱网格（时间元J与空间元K的张量积，满足|J|与diam(K)²成比例）；在空间二维域上进行数值实验验证。

Result: 自适应算法相比均匀网格细化显著加快收敛速度，在强奇异性存在下仍能达到接近最优的收敛速率。

Conclusion: 所提出的加权残差后验误差估计器能够有效驱动自适应时空边界元算法，实现高效的热方程数值求解。

Abstract: We consider the space-time boundary element method (BEM) for the heat equation with prescribed initial and Neumann data. We propose a weighted-residual a posteriori error estimator that is an upper bound for the unknown BEM error. The possibly locally refined meshes are assumed to be parabolically scaled prismatic, i.e., their elements are tensor-products $J\times K$ of elements in time $J$ and space $K$ with $|J| \eqsim \text{diam}(K)^2$. In the considered numerical experiments on two-dimensional domains in space, an adaptive algorithm steered by the derived estimator yields significantly faster convergence compared to uniform refinement, achieving near-optimal rates even in the presence of strong singularities.

</details>


### [113] [Error Analysis of a Fully-Discrete Implicit $θ$-Scheme with WG-FEM for Parabolic Singularly Perturbed Boundary Turning Point Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13611)
*Aayushman Raina,Srinivasan Natesan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对一类抛物型奇异摄动边界转向问题，提出了一种弱Galerkin有限元方法，采用隐式θ格式时间离散和Shishkin网格空间离散，证明了稳定性和参数一致收敛性，并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决抛物型奇异摄动边界转向点在数值求解中存在的边界层效应和参数摄动导致的收敛困难问题，现有方法在处理此类问题时空耦合和高梯度区域时精度不足。

Method: 采用弱Galerkin有限元法进行空间离散（基于Shishkin层适应网格），采用隐式θ格式进行时间离散（均匀网格），建立了半离散和全离散格式的稳定性分析与能量范数误差估计。

Result: 证明了所提格式关于摄动参数的一致收敛性，推导了能量范数下的误差估计，数值实验验证了理论结果和计算效率。

Conclusion: 所建立的WG-FEM框架有效处理了抛物型奇异摄动边界转向问题，且为未来向高维问题扩展（如ADI型算子分裂WG-FEM格式）奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: In this article, we introduce a weak Galerkin finite element method (WG-FEM) for a class of parabolic singularly perturbed boundary turning point problems (SPBTPPs). The proposed numerical scheme employs an implicit $θ$-scheme for temporal discretization over a uniform mesh and applies WG-FEM spatial discretization on a layer-adapted Shishkin mesh. Rigorous stability estimates are established for both the semi-discrete and fully-discrete formulations. Furthermore, we derive error estimates in the energy norm and prove that the convergence of the scheme is uniform with respect to the perturbation parameter. Numerical tests are conducted to verify the theoretical findings and illustrate the efficiency of the proposed method. In addition, the theoretical framework developed in this work lays the foundation for future extensions to higher-dimensional problems using ADI-type operator splitting WG-FEM schemes.

</details>


### [114] [Revisiting column subset selection through the lens of submodularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13823)
*Ilse C. F. Ipsen,Arvind K. Saibaba*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明从实矩阵中选取最大体积的k列等价于最大化一个子模函数，进而分析了Businger-Golub QR列主元消去（一种贪心算法）和Gu-Eisenstat强秩揭示QR（一种1-交换算法）的相对误差界，前者不超过37%，后者不超过50%，并推广至对称正定矩阵的子矩阵体积最大化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究从实矩阵中选取具有最大体积的k列这一经典问题，并解释为何实践中简单高效的Businger-Golub QR列主元消去法效果优异。

Method: 将列体积的对数视为列集合上的子模函数，证明当矩阵满秩且奇异值足够大时该函数非负非递减；将Businger-Golub QR解释为贪心算法，Gu-Eisenstat QR解释为1-交换算法，并推导相对误差界；进一步推广到对称正定矩阵的子矩阵体积最大化。

Result: Businger-Golub QR的相对误差不超过37%，Gu-Eisenstat QR的相对误差不超过50%，前者在该度量下更优。对于一般矩阵，两种方法分别对应贪心和1-交换算法以最大化QR分解中上三角矩阵的迹。

Conclusion: 子模函数视角统一了矩阵体积最大化问题的算法分析，理论证明了简单QR列主元法的高效性。

Abstract: The problem is to select k columns with maximal volume from a real mxn matrix X. We show that the logarithm of the volume is a submodular function on columns of X, and for full column-rank matrices X with sufficiently large singular values, it is a non-negative non-decreasing function. As a consequence, traditional Businger-Golub QR with column pivoting is a greedy algorithm, with a relative error of at most 37 percent. In contrast, Gu-Eisenstat strong rank-revealing QR is a 1-interchange algorithm, with a relative error of at most 50 percent. The higher accuracy, under this metric, of the simple QR with column pivoting confirms its well known effectiveness in practice. For general matrices, we show that Businger-Golub QR is a greedy algorithm, and Gu-Eisenstat QR a 1-interchange algorithm for maximizing the trace of the upper triangular matrix in a QR factorization of X, with Businger-Golub again having a better error bound. This is extended to finding kxk submatrices of maximal volume in symmetric positive-definite matrices.

</details>


### [115] [An Order-One Lower Bound on the Error of Scalable Generalized Multiscale Finite Element Space Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13888)
*Changqing Ye*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于具有粗糙系数的椭圆方程，任何满足固定支撑半径、系数信息半径和局部多重性界限的确定性基函数构造，都无法在系数类上实现一致收敛；其最坏情况下的Galerkin误差被一个与粗尺度H无关的正常数所下界限制。因此，要实现一致最优精度，至少需要一个局部构造参数随H增长，或需要超出固定可见性模型的系数信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MsFEM虽然具有类似FEM的局部构造，但现有分析无法在整个有界对比可测系数类上给出系数一致的O(H)能量估计。为弥合这一理论空白，本文形式化了一种类似FEM的"结构可扩展性"概念，并探究其局限性。

Method: 采用反证法：构造一个固定的有限族光滑周期系数和光滑右端项；利用局部斑块上系数重合的策略、校正子场的有限维逼近下界、正密度网格论证以及强周期校正子收敛性，建立误差下界。

Result: 证明任何具有固定支撑半径、系数信息半径和局部多重性界限的确定性局部基构造，其最坏情况下的L²-能量Galerkin误差被一个与H无关的正常数下界限制，即无法实现一致收敛。

Conclusion: 实现一致最优精度要么需要至少一个局部构造参数（如局部化半径或局部谱维数）随粗尺度H→0增长，要么需要超出固定可见性模型的系数信息（如全局或准全局信息）。

Abstract: Several coefficient-adapted methods provide optimal-order approximation for elliptic equations with rough coefficients. Prominent examples include localized orthogonal decomposition, multiscale spectral GFEM, and constraint energy-minimizing GMsFEM. Their proven accuracy, however, is obtained by allowing the localization radius or the local spectral dimension to grow as the coarse scale \(H\) tends to zero. Classical MsFEM has an FEM-like local construction, but its available analysis does not give a coefficient-uniform \(\bigO(H)\) energy estimate over the full bounded-contrast measurable coefficient class. Motivated by this gap, we formalize an FEM-like notion of structural scalability. A chosen spatially local basis has uniformly bounded overlap, hence \(\bigO(1)\) stiffness entries per row, and every anchored local span uses coefficient information from only \(\bigO(1)\) coarse-element layers. We prove that no deterministic construction satisfying fixed bounds on the support radius, coefficient-information radius, and local multiplicity can converge uniformly over the coefficient class. In fact, its worst-case \(L^2\)-to-energy Galerkin error remains bounded below by a positive constant independent of \(H\). The lower bound is established using a fixed finite family of smooth periodic coefficients and smooth right-hand sides. The proof combines coefficients that coincide on local patches, a finite-dimensional approximation lower bound for corrector fields, a positive-density mesh argument, and strong periodic corrector convergence. Thus uniform optimal accuracy requires at least one local construction parameter to grow or requires coefficient information beyond the fixed-visibility model.

</details>


### [116] [Adaptivity in Local Kernel Based Methods for Approximating Solutions to the Poisson Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14057)
*Jonah A. Reeger,Anders R. Johnson,Shelby W. Woodrum*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文基于自适应局部核方法，开发了用于泊松方程求解的局部误差估计与自适应过程。该方法本质无网格，利用局部Delaunay三角剖分获取节点几何信息后即丢弃。实验显示误差估计与真实误差高度吻合，相较现有细化指标有优势，能自动聚焦局部特征而避免全局均匀细化的高昂代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自适应局部核方法近似线性算子作用的最新进展基础上，需要一种能够自动定位并细化求解域中误差较大区域的策略，以高效求解泊松方程，避免全局均匀细化带来的难以承受的计算开销。

Method: 提出局部误差估计量，在自适应过程中用于判定哪些区域需要通过减小节点间距来降低误差。方法本质无网格，仅临时借助局部Delaunay三角剖分获取节点局部几何信息，使用后即丢弃。

Result: 实验表明所提误差估计与近似解的实际绝对前向误差高度一致，且优于现有的细化指示器。自适应过程与误差估计的组合能够自动聚焦并解析解的局部特征。

Conclusion: 该自适应方法与误差估计提供了一种自动化的求解技术，能够在避免全局均匀细化高昂代价的前提下，有效解析解的局部特征。

Abstract: Expanding on the recent development of adaptive local kernel methods for approximating the action of linear operators, a local estimate of the error and an adaptive procedure for approximating solutions to the Poisson equation is developed. The error estimate is used in the midst of the adaptive procedure to determine locations in the solution domain where decreasing the spacing between nodes can decrease the error in the solution. The approach described here is essentially "meshless", with only local Delaunay triangulations leveraged as a convenience to provide information on the local geometry of the node set. Once this information is utilized, it is discarded. The experiments performed show close agreement between the error estimate and actual absolute forward error in the approximate solution along with a comparison to existing indicators for refinement. The combination of the adaptive procedure and error estimate provides an automated technique to resolve localized features of a solution without the, often intractable, expense of uniform refinement across the entire solution domain.

</details>
