### [117] [On the Second Moment of $ L (1/2, \mathrm{As}(f) \times φ)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13484)
*Changlin Li,Zhi Qi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在实二次域上建立了Asai提升的大筛子不等式，并应用该不等式证明了卷积中心L值的第二矩在权方向上的非平凡界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究实二次域上Hilbert尖形式的Asai提升，并建立其大筛子不等式，进而用于研究相关L值的分析性质。

Method: 利用Hecke正交基和平行权Hilbert模尖形式空间，建立Asai提升的大筛子不等式；结合固定Hecke-Maass尖形式，分析卷积中心L值的第二矩。

Result: 建立了Asai提升的大筛子不等式，并得到了卷积L值第二矩在k-方面的非平凡上界估计。

Conclusion: 大筛子不等式可作为研究L值分布的有效工具，所得非平凡界改进了平凡估计。

Abstract: Let $\mathbf{F} = \mathbf{Q}(\sqrt D)$ be a real quadratic field. In this paper, we establish a large sieve inequality for the Asai lifts $ \mathrm{As} (f) $ with $f$ in a Hecke orthonormal basis of the space of Hilbert modular cusp forms of parallel weight $(k, k)$ over $ \mathbf{F} $. As an application, for a fixed Hecke--Maass cusp form $φ$ over $\mathbf{Q}$, we prove a non-trivial bound for the second moment of the convoluted central $L$-values $ L (1/2, \mathrm{As}(f) \times φ) $ in the $k$-aspect.

</details>


### [118] [Study on Morgan-Voyce type polynomials with Euler-Seidel algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13489)
*Taekyun Kim,Dae san Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文融合退化特殊多项式、Euler-Seidel矩阵方法和Morgan-Voyce多项式，引入两类新型Morgan-Voyce型多项式，建立其显式公式和递推关系，定义三个多项式变体，证明Bell多项式的三类二项式型和可用这些新族的有穷和表示，并推导其指数生成函数，最后利用Morgan型多项式的初始序列构造Euler-Seidel矩阵，得到新的代数恒等式并扩展矩阵方法在组合分析中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将退化特殊多项式、Euler-Seidel矩阵方法与Morgan-Voyce多项式这三个领域进行桥接，探索它们之间的深层联系，并推广Morgan-Voyce多项式的理论框架。

Method: 引入两类新的Morgan-Voyce型多项式族，推导其显式公式和递推关系；定义三个多项式变体；利用这些新族将Bell多项式的三类二项式型和表示为有穷和；推导指数生成函数；基于Morgan型多项式的初始序列构造Euler-Seidel矩阵。

Result: 建立了新型Morgan-Voyce多项式的结构性质，给出了Bell多项式二项式型和的有限和表达式及指数生成函数，构建了相应的Euler-Seidel矩阵，导出了一系列新的代数恒等式。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将三个不同数学领域联系起来，所得结果丰富了代数恒等式的理论体系，并拓展了矩阵方法在组合分析中的应用范围。

Abstract: This paper bridges the domains of degenerate special polynomials, the Euler-Seidel matrix method, and Morgan-Voyce polynomials. We introduce two new families of Morgan-Voyce type polynomials and establish their structural properties, including explicit formulas and recurrence relations. Additionally, we define three polynomial variants and prove that three distinct binomial-type sums for Bell polynomials can be expressed as finite sums involving these new families, and derive their exponential generating functions. We then construct Euler-Seidel matrices using the initial sequences associated with Morgan-type polynomials. Our results yield novel algebraic identities and expand the application of matrix methods in combinatorial analysis.

</details>


### [119] [On the Second Moment of $L (1/2, \mathrm{As} (f))$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13490)
*Changlin Li,Zhi Qi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了实二次域上全水平Hilbert尖形式族在平行权趋于无穷时，中心Asai L-值第二矩的渐近公式，结果精确到主项，改进了Luo的均值Lindelöf界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在解析数论与自守形式中，理解L-函数矩的渐近行为是核心问题之一。先前Luo已证明了均值Lindelöf界O(k^{2+ε})，本文旨在获得更精确的渐近公式，揭示二阶矩的主项结构。

Method: 利用Hecke正交基的调和权重，结合自守形式的谱分解、Kuznetsov迹公式及Weyl律等工具，对Asai L-值进行矩分析，通过精细的解析估计得到主项与余项。

Result: 得到中心Asai L-值第二矩的渐近公式：求和项等于一个显式3次多项式P_3(log k)乘以k^2，余项为O_{F,ε}(k^{3/2+ε})，将误差从Luo的O(k^{2+ε})改进至k^{3/2+ε}量级。

Conclusion: 该结果不仅证实了在实二次域全水平Hilbert尖形式族上Asai L-值二阶矩的精确增长阶为k^2 (log k)^3，且为后续更高矩或更一般自守L-函数的矩研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Let $\mathbf{F}$ be a real quadratic field. Let $f $ traverse a Hecke orthonormal basis of Hilbert cusp forms over $ \mathbf{F} $ of full level and parallel weight $(k,k)$. As $k \rightarrow \infty$, we prove an asymptotic formula for the second moment of central Asai $L$-values $L (1/2, \mathrm{As} (f))$:
  \begin{equation*}
  {\sum}_{f } \, ω_f L(1/2,\mathrm{As}(f))^2 = P_3 ( \log {k } ) k^2 + O_{\mathbf{F},\varepsilon} (k^{3/2 + \varepsilon} ),
  \end{equation*}
  where $ω_f$ are the harmonic weights and $P_3 (X)$ is an explicit polynomial of degree $3$. This refines the mean Lindelöf bound $ O_{\mathbf{F},\varepsilon} (k^{2 + \varepsilon} ) $ proved by Wenzhi Luo.

</details>


### [120] [Torsion groups of rational elliptic curves over $\mathbb{Z}_p$-extensions of quadratic fields: the $p\le 5$ case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13514)
*Haidong Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文推广了Avcı定理，证明当L替换为K的所有Z_p-扩张的复合域K_∞时，对p=5有E(K)_tors = E(K_∞)_tors成立，并对p=3和p=2给出部分结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Avcı关于二次域K上Z_p-扩张中椭圆曲线挠子群不变性的定理，将其从单个Z_p-扩张L推广到所有Z_p-扩张的复合域K_∞。

Method: 将Avcí定理中的Z_p-扩张L替换为K的所有Z_p-扩张的复合域K_∞，分析该设定下挠子群的结构，并针对不同素数p分别处理。

Result: 在K_∞设定下，对p=5完全证明了E(K)_tors = E(K_∞)_tors；对p=3和p=2仅得到部分结果。

Conclusion: Avcı定理可推广到所有Z_p-扩张的复合域情形，但推广程度依赖于素数p，对p=5完全成立，对更小素数仅部分成立。

Abstract: Let $E$ be a rational elliptic curve. We generalize a theorem due to Avcı\cite{AVCI2026153}, which asserts that for any quadratic field \(K\) and prime \(p>5\), the equality \(E(K)_{\mathrm{tors}} = E(L)_{\mathrm{tors}}\) holds for every \(\mathbb{Z}_p\)-extension \(L/K\). In this paper, we consider the setting where the \(\mathbb{Z}_p\)-extension \(L\) is replaced by the compositum \(K_{\infty}\) of all \(\mathbb{Z}_p\)-extensions of \(K\). Under this new setting, we prove the analogous statement for \(p=5\), and further provide partial results for the remaining primes \(p=3\) and \(p=2\).

</details>


### [121] [Spectral fourth moments of Hecke--Maaß cusp forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13518)
*Edgar Assing,Peter Humphries*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了GL(2)自守形式在Heegner点处第四矩的最优界，并应用于移位卷积问题和Diophantine指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为自守形式的谱矩建立本质上最优的界，并探索其在数论问题中的应用。

Method: 考虑Hecke-Maaß尖形式的族（谱参数在二进区间内），研究这些形式在Heegner点处的第四矩。

Result: 得到了自守形式在Heegner点处第四矩的最优界。

Conclusion: 该结果可用于解决涉及平方和函数r(n)的移位卷积问题，以及点态Diophantine指数问题。

Abstract: In this note, we establish essentially optimal bounds for certain spectral moments of automorphic forms for $\mathrm{GL}(2)$. More precisely, we consider the family of Hecke--Maaß cusp forms with spectral parameter in a dyadic interval and study the fourth moment of these forms evaluated at a Heegner point. We additionally present applications of our main result to the shifted convolution problem involving the sum of two squares function $r(n)$ as well as to pointwise Diophantine exponents.

</details>


### [122] [Bernoulli determinants and cuspidal subgroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13536)
*François Brunault*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了模曲线X_1(N)上有理尖点类群阶的显式公式，该公式适用于任意整数N。证明依赖于Streng关于X_1(N)上模单位群的结果，并需要计算涉及第二伯努利多项式的某个行列式。此外，本文还定义了尖点类群的高权类比，并推测其阶与使用更高次伯努利多项式定义的类似行列式有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为任意整数N的模曲线X_1(N)上的有理尖点类群阶提供显式公式，并探索高权情形的推广。

Method: 基于Streng关于模单位群的结果，通过计算涉及第二伯努利多项式的行列式来推导公式。

Result: 得到了有理尖点类群阶的显式公式，并定义了高权类比。

Conclusion: 成功给出了有理尖点类群阶的显式公式，并推测高权类比与更高次伯努利多项式定义的行列式相关。

Abstract: We give an explicit formula for the order of the rational cuspidal class group of the modular curve $X_1(N)$ for an arbitrary integer $N$. The proof relies on results of Streng on the group of modular units on $X_1(N)$, and requires computing a certain determinant involving the second Bernoulli polynomial. We also define a higher weight analogue of the cuspidal class group and speculate that its order is related to a similar determinant defined using a higher degree Bernoulli polynomial.

</details>


### [123] [Generalization of Ramanujan's Continued Fractions for Even Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13600)
*Dipika Sarkar,S. N. Fathima,M. P. Thejitha*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用Ramanujan的一般连分数恒等式推导出任意偶数阶k的三个广义连分数，并建立了这些连分数的广义theta函数恒等式。作为76阶连分数的应用，获得了分拆理论恒等式和一些消失系数结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Ramanujan连分数恒等式的推广形式，建立高阶连分数与theta函数之间的关联，并挖掘其在数论分拆理论中的应用潜力。

Method: 基于Ramanujan的一般连分数恒等式，推导任意偶数阶k的广义连分数；利用theta函数理论建立这些连分数的恒等式；通过76阶连分数实例，推导分拆理论恒等式和消失系数结果。

Result: 得到了任意偶数阶k的三个广义连分数及其对应的theta函数恒等式；针对76阶连分数，获得了具体分拆理论恒等式及部分系数消失的结果。

Conclusion: 成功将Ramanujan连分数推广至任意偶数阶，建立了与theta函数的内在联系，并在高阶情况下（如76阶）发现了有意义的分拆理论性质。

Abstract: In this paper, we derive three generalized continued fractions of any even order $k$ with the aid of a general continued fraction identity of Ramanujan and we establish general theta function identities for these continued fractions. As an application of continued fraction of order seventy-six, we obtain partition theoretic identities and some vanishing coefficient results.

</details>


### [124] [Representability of systems of proportionally modular numerical semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13619)
*Zsolt Baja,Tamás László,Zsuzsa Nagy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了每个比例模数值半群系统都可以通过加权齐次曲面奇点的规范等变解耦来表示，该奇点具有有理同调球链接。构造从比例模数值半群的双生成数值半群商描述出发，通过双支规范等变解图实现每个商，然后用适当的多重性将这些图粘合起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立比例模数值半群系统与加权齐次曲面奇点规范等变解耦表示之间的对应关系，将代数结构（半群）与几何结构（奇点解耦图）联系起来。

Method: 1) 从比例模数值半群的双生成数值半群商描述出发；2) 通过双支规范等变解图实现每个商；3) 用适当的多重性将这些图粘合起来。

Result: 证明了每个比例模数值半群系统都可以通过加权齐次曲面奇点的规范等变解耦来表示，且该奇点具有有理同调球链接。

Conclusion: 比例模数值半群系统与加权齐次曲面奇点的规范等变解耦图之间存在严格的构造性对应关系。

Abstract: In this short note we prove that every system of proportionally modular numerical semigroups is representable by a canonical equivariant resolution of a weighted homogeneous surface singularity with rational homology sphere link. The construction starts from the quotient descriptions of proportionally modular numerical semigroups by two-generator numerical semigroups, realizes each quotient by a two-legged canonical equivariant resolution graph, and then glues these graphs with suitable multiplicities.

</details>


### [125] [Area correlations related to lattice points in discs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13659)
*Matteo Bordignon,Pär Kurlberg*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了环面上圆流的面积观测量的混合性质，证明尽管该流本质上是可积的，但仍具有混合性，并确定了面积概率密度函数在全局和局部范围内的分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Lester-Wigman关于圆边界附近格点消失面积相关猜想的启发，本文旨在探究环面上圆流（与带电粒子在环面磁场中的运动相关）的面积相关性行为。

Method: 通过分析环面上圆流的动力学特性，研究面积观测量的混合性质，并推导全局与局部范围内的概率密度函数。

Result: 证明了尽管环面圆流本质上是可积系统，但其面积观测量仍具有混合性质（即消失相关猜想在该设定下成立）；同时给出了面积在全局和局部范围内的概率密度函数。

Conclusion: 环面上的圆流虽可积，但面积观测量依然表现出混合行为，这为相关猜想提供了支持，并给出了完整的概率分布刻画。

Abstract: Motivated by the Lester-Wigman vanishing area correlation conjecture for lattice points near the boundary of circles of growing radius, we investigate the dynamics of circle flows on tori (which are related to the motion of a charged particle in a magnetic field on a torus.) We show that an analogue of the vanishing correlation conjecture holds in this setting, i.e., we have "mixing for the area observable" despite the flow being essentially integrable. We also determine the probability density function of the areas, in global as well as local regimes.

</details>


### [126] [The mean value of the digits of $1/p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13663)
*Kurt Girstmair*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文解决了素数倒数在基数b下展开时，周期长度为奇数情形下的数字均值计算问题，给出了统一的公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作已给出周期长度为偶数时数字均值即为(b-1)l/2，以及奇数长度l=(p-1)/2^m时的结果，但该结果涉及广义伯努利数，且对于任意奇数长度l的情形尚不明确，本文旨在解决这一遗留问题。

Method: 利用数论中的乘法阶概念，将周期长度l定义为b模p的乘法阶，并针对奇数l的情形进行数学推导，统一处理任意奇数长度的数字均值。

Result: 给出了任意奇数周期长度l下，1/p在基数b展开中一个周期内数字均值的显式公式。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了素数倒数在基b展开中周期长度为奇数时数字均值的计算问题，填补了此前工作的空白。

Abstract: Let $p\ge 3$ be a prime and $b\ge 2$ an integer such that $p$ does not divide $b$. Then $1/p$ has a periodic digit expansion with respect to the basis $b$. The length $l$ of the period is the (multiplicative) order of $b$ mod $p$. If $l$ is even, then the mean value of the digits of a period is just $(b-1)l/2$. The case of an odd length $l$ is more interesting. If $l=(p-1)/2^m$ is odd, the mean value of the digits of a period was given previously. This mean value involves generalized Bernoulli numbers. However, it is not clear how this result can be generalized to an arbitrary odd length $l$. In the present note we settle this case.

</details>


### [127] [Exact classification of elliptic curves $y^{2}=x^{3}-pqx$ with rank $0$ and trivial $\Sha[2]$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14033)
*Arkabrata Ghosh,Paul M. Voutier*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了椭圆曲线 $E_{p,q}: y^{2}=x^{3}-pqx$（$p$和$q$为不同奇素数），建立了其有理点秩 rank$\,E_{p,q}(\mathbb{Q})$ 和 2-Selmer 群 $\dim_{\mathbb{F}_{2}} \Sha \left( E_{p,q}/\bbQ \right)[2]$ 同时为零的充要条件。该条件通过刻画与度-2同源 $\varphi$ 及其对偶 $\widehat{\varphi}$ 相关的 Selmer 群均取最小规模的等价条件，并结合一个相关正合序列中余核的性质得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为特定形式的椭圆曲线 $E_{p,q}: y^{2}=x^{3}-pqx$ 提供秩和 2-部分 Tate-Shafarevich 群同时为零的充要条件，从而深入理解这类曲线的算术性质。

Method: 利用度-2同源 $\varphi$ 及其对偶 $\widehat{\varphi}$ 的 Selmer 群的最小规模刻画，结合相关正合序列中余核的分析，建立充要条件。

Result: 给出了 rank$\,E_{p,q}(\mathbb{Q})$ 和 $\dim_{\mathbb{F}_{2}} \Sha \left( E_{p,q}/\bbQ \right)[2]$ 同时为零的充要条件。

Conclusion: 通过同源 Selmer 群的最小性及相关余核性质，完整刻画了此类椭圆曲线秩和 2-部分 Tate-Shafarevich 群均为零的情形。

Abstract: For the elliptic curves $E_{p,q}: y^{2}=x^{3}-pqx$ where $p$ and $q$ are distinct odd primes, we establish necessary and sufficient conditions under which rank$\,E_{p,q}(\mathbb{Q})$ and $\dim_{\mathbb{F}_{2}} \Sha \left( E_{p,q}/\bbQ \right)[2]$ are both $0$. We do so via a similar characterisation of when the Selmer groups associated with the degree-$2$ isogeny $φ$ and its dual $\widehatφ$ are both of minimal size, along with results about a cokernel that arises from a related exact sequence.

</details>


### [128] [Strongly complete sets and a conjecture of Erdős](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14071)
*Steve Fan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了当集合A在二进区间(2^k,2^{k+1}]中至少包含6个元素，且对任意无理数θ，A中元素的小数部分加权和发散时，A是强完备的，从而解决了Erdős在1961年提出的一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Erdős于1961年提出的关于强完备集合的猜想，完善整数子集完备性与结构之间的关系理论。

Method: 在Bergelson和Simmons前期工作基础上，利用二进区间上的密度条件和θ的小数部分分布的发散性条件，通过构造适当有序块来建立更一般的强完备性判据。

Result: 证明了：若A∩(2^k,2^{k+1}]中元素个数≥6（对充分大的k），且∑_{a∈A}‖aθ‖=∞对任意θ∈ℝ\ℤ成立，则A是强完备的。该结果解决了Erdős猜想，并可推广到更一般的块状结构。

Conclusion: 该结果不仅解决了经典猜想，还建立了更为通用的强完备性判据框架，对理解整数集的加性结构有重要意义。

Abstract: A set $A\subseteq\mathbb{N}$ is called $\textit{complete}$ if every sufficiently large integer can be written as a sum of distinct elements of $A$. It is $\textit{strongly complete}$ if it remains complete after one deletes finitely many elements from it. We show that $A\subseteq\mathbb{N}$ is strongly complete whenever \[
  \big|A\cap(2^k,2^{k+1}]\big|\ge6 \] for every sufficiently large $k\in\mathbb{N}$, and \[
  \sum_{a\in A}\|aθ\|=\infty,
  \quad\forallθ\in\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathbb{Z}. \] In particular, this resolves a 1961 conjecture of Erdős. The proof builds on previous work of Bergelson and Simmons. Our approach also allows us to establish a more general strong-completeness criterion with suitable ordered blocks in place of dyadic intervals.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Overcoming slow Kolmogorov width decay in parametric optimal control via neural network surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13226)
*Hendrik Kleikamp,Martin Lazar,Juan Ricardo Muñoz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对参数化线性二次最优控制问题，提出基于U-Net的非线性替代模型来近似最优终端时间伴随状态，克服传统线性降阶模型在Kolmogorov宽度衰减缓慢时的效率瓶颈，并通过后验误差估计保证结果的可信性，数值实验表明U-Net在精度和训练效率上均优于现有线性与非线性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在参数化线性二次最优控制问题中，当参数依赖的目标状态导致终端时间伴随状态的Kolmogorov宽度衰减缓慢时，传统线性降阶模型需要很大的降阶空间才能保证足够精度，效率低下。因此需要寻找能够克服线性模型局限性的非线性替代方法。

Method: 以热方程分布控制为例，先建立理论结果证明参数空间上终端时间伴随流形的Kolmogorov宽度衰减缓慢（若目标状态本身如此）。然后提出基于U-Net的非线性替代模型，将参数场映射到近似终端时间伴随状态。并证明合适的后验误差估计器可适用于U-Net近似，用于认证替代结果。

Result: 通过两项广泛的数值实验，U-Net替代模型在精度上始终优于文献中的多种线性与非线性方法，且所需训练样本显著更少。

Conclusion: U-Net能够有效地克服线性降阶模型在Kolmogorov宽度衰减缓慢问题中的效率瓶颈，在保证精度的同时大幅降低训练需求，是一种有潜力的参数化最优控制问题非线性替代方案。

Abstract: In this paper we deal with parametric, linear-quadratic optimal control problems in which the solution can be uniquely characterized by the optimal final time adjoint state. As a motivating example, we establish theoretical results showing that for distributed control of the heat equation, the manifold of final time adjoints over the parameter space exhibits a slow decay of its Kolmogorov width if this was already the case for the parameter-dependent target states. Traditional linear reduced-order models would thus require a large reduced space in order to guarantee a sufficient accuracy, making them inefficient in this application. To overcome the limitation of linear models, we discuss a nonlinear surrogate based on U-Nets that maps parametric fields to approximate final time adjoints. We show that a suitable a posteriori error estimator remains applicable to the U-Net approximation and can be used to certify the surrogate results. Through two extensive numerical experiments, we show the potential of the U-Net surrogate and compare it with several linear and nonlinear methods from the literature. The results show that the U-Net consistently achieves the highest accuracy among the methods considered while requiring significantly fewer training samples.

</details>


### [130] [Design of Carbon Capture Processes Under Part-load Operating Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13232)
*David Y. Shu,Boxun Huang,Yurim Kim,Randall Field,Rahul Gandhi,Sungho Shin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于随机优化的数据驱动方法，通过考虑燃煤电厂部分负荷工况下烟气条件的变异性来优化溶剂法碳捕集过程设计，可在增加运营成本的同时降低设备尺寸和总成本6-9%，碳捕集总成本降低0.7-1.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可调度发电厂需满足变化负荷需求，碳捕集过程设计需考虑多个运行点，但多运行点优化计算复杂度高，实践中常基于单运行点设计，忽略了烟气条件的变异性对设备尺寸和成本的影响。

Method: 采用随机优化方法，结合数据驱动的吸收/解吸过程趋近平衡模型，基于典型燃煤电厂部分负荷运行数据表征可变烟气条件，在降低计算复杂度的同时优化碳捕集过程设计。

Result: 考虑烟气条件变异性可大幅降低设备尺寸和总电厂成本6-9%，虽运营成本有所增加，但碳捕集总成本降低0.7-1.7%。

Conclusion: 鉴于碳捕集的资本密集特性，设计阶段应考虑烟气条件变异性，尤其是对参与负荷跟踪的电厂部署碳捕集时更为重要。

Abstract: Solvent-based carbon capture can reduce CO2 emissions resulting from a continued reliance on fossil power plants for firm power. These capture processes remove CO2 from flue gases via a solvent. Careful design via process systems optimization can limit the overall cost of carbon capture, which is both capital- and energy intensive. As dispatchable power plants operate to meet varying load demand, the design process needs to account for varying operating points. However, optimizing the design over multiple operating points yields high computational complexity, which is why designs are often based on a single operating point in practice. Here, we identify optimal carbon capture process designs via stochastic optimization, reducing computational complexity through a data-driven approach-to-equilibrium model of the absorption and desorption processes. We represent variable flue gas conditions based on part-load operation data of a representative coal power plant. Accounting for this variability in the design substantially reduces equipment size and total plant cost by 6-9 % at the expense higher operating costs, yielding a reduction in total cost of carbon capture by 0.7-1.7 %. Given the capital intensity of carbon capture, variability of flue gas conditions therefore should be considered at the design stage, particularly if capture is deployed on plants subject to load following.

</details>


### [131] [An ALNS Heuristic for Large-Scale Line Planning with Mode Choice and Line Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13263)
*Siv Marie Cartland Hansen,Richard Martin Lusby,Rowan Hoogervorst,Otto Anker Nielsen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的自适应大邻域搜索（ALNS）算法，用于解决考虑内生需求的大规模公共交通线路规划问题。该算法在丹麦欧登塞的公共交通网络上进行了评估（约1800个OD对），通过联合优化线路和发车频率，将平均发车间隔从约41分钟降至6.8-13分钟，同时大幅提升了公共交通客流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求响应性是公共交通线路规划中的重要考量因素，因为网络设计和服务质量会影响乘客需求。然而，考虑这种相互作用使得本已具有挑战性的组合优化问题更加复杂。现有方法难以有效处理大规模实例中线路规划与内生需求之间的耦合关系。

Method: 提出可扩展的自适应大邻域搜索（ALNS）算法，动态生成候选线路，集成乘客分配与需求估计的评估过程，以及专门的频率优化局部搜索过程，实现线路与频率的联合优化。

Result: 在丹麦欧登塞约1800个OD对的真实交通网络上验证了方法的适用性。优化后的网络将资源集中在少数高频率服务上，平均发车间隔从约41分钟降至6.8-13分钟，公共交通客流量显著增加。网络设计对乘客行为假设高度敏感。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效处理大规模现实线路规划问题；需求响应性线路规划中需仔细标定需求模型，因为乘客行为假设会显著影响最优网络设计。

Abstract: Demand responsiveness is an important consideration in public transport line planning, as network design and service quality influence passenger demand. However, accounting for this interaction further complicates an already challenging combinatorial optimization problem. To address this challenge, we propose a scalable Adaptive Large Neighborhood Search (ALNS) algorithm for large-scale line planning with endogenous demand. The algorithm jointly optimizes lines and frequencies while accounting for passenger mode choice, passenger assignment, and vehicle capacities. Candidate lines are generated dynamically throughout the search, and solutions are evaluated using an embedded evaluation procedure for passenger assignment and demand estimation, together with a dedicated local search procedure for frequency optimization. The proposed methodology is evaluated on the public transport network of Odense, Denmark, comprising approximately 1,800 origin-destination pairs. Computational results demonstrate the applicability of the approach to realistic, large-scale instances. The optimized networks concentrate resources on fewer, higher-frequency services, reducing average headways from approximately 41 minutes to 6.8-13 minutes while substantially increasing public transport ridership. Furthermore, the results show that network design is highly sensitive to assumptions regarding passenger behavior, highlighting the importance of carefully calibrated demand models when incorporating demand responsiveness into line planning.

</details>


### [132] [Model predictive control for laser thermal processing: operator learning, closed-loop validation, and out-of-distribution analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13289)
*Smajil Halilovic,Yuchen Zhang,Daniel M. Tartakovsky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对激光粉末床熔融等热加工过程中峰值温度控制问题，开发了一种基于深度算子网络的替代模型控制管道，通过多步预测和模型预测控制实现快速精确的温度调控，并验证了离线精度并非控制就绪性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光热加工中，移动热源重新进入已加热材料区域时会产生热积累，导致峰值温度超出工艺窗口并引发缺陷。高保真热模型虽能准确捕捉物理现象但计算速度过慢，无法用于在线优化，因此需要快速、可微且可泛化的替代模型。

Method: 开发了一种专为移动热源设计的多步深度算子网络：分支子网络编码未来功率与轨迹（位置和速度）序列，主干编码当前峰值温度及未来激光位置温度，实现单次五步预测。将该替代模型作为平滑非线性规划嵌入到基于CasADi/IPOPT的滚动时域模型预测控制器中。

Result: 替代模型前向传播比等效有限差分步快千倍以上；具有几乎相同离线误差的两个替代模型在闭环中表现截然不同；受控双集成数据设计将路径拐角欠预测失败从91K降至1.4K；校准的单侧约束余量13K在所有测试路径上实现了真实上限零违规。

Conclusion: 集合开环精度对控制就绪性是必要但不充分的；离线精度相近的模型在闭环控制中可能有显著差异；通过适当的数据设计和约束余量校准可实现可靠的在线温度控制。

Abstract: Laser-based thermal processing, such as laser powder bed fusion, requires tight regulation of the peak surface temperature: heat accumulates where the moving source re-enters previously heated material, driving the temperature out of its process window and causing defects. High-fidelity thermal models capture this physics but are too slow for online optimization, which motivates fast, differentiable, and generalizable surrogates. We develop and validate a complete surrogate-based control pipeline that regulates the maximum surface temperature of a moving laser on a 304-stainless-steel substrate. We also determine conditions under which our surrogate can be trusted inside the control loop by probing its out-of-distribution limits. A key component of our surrogate is a multi-step deep operator network bespoke for moving sources: its branch subnetwork encodes the future power and trajectory (position and velocity) sequence, while its trunk encodes the current peak temperature and the temperature at the future laser locations, yielding a one-shot five-step prediction. By way of illustration, we use this surrogate as a smooth (algebraic-rectifier) nonlinear program inside a receding-horizon model predictive controller solved in CasADi/IPOPT. The surrogate forward pass is over thousand times faster than the equivalent finite-difference steps. We show that aggregate open-loop accuracy is necessary but not sufficient for control-readiness: two surrogates with near-identical offline error behave drastically differently in closed loop. A controlled two-ensemble data design reduces a 91 K path-corner underprediction failure to 1.4 K, and a calibrated one-sided constraint margin of 13 K yields zero violations of the true upper bound on all tested paths.

</details>


### [133] [State constrained convex Nash equilibrium problems coupled with linear hyperbolic PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13299)
*Marcelo Bongarti,Michael Hintermüller*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了与双曲型偏微分方程耦合的状态约束凸广义纳什均衡问题，解决了因策略-状态映射紧性不足导致的长期存在性理论空白，并给出了线性双曲PDE的一阶最优性条件及在波动方程、广告动力学和网络上的线性化等温欧拉系统等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆和抛物型PDE耦合的GNEP存在性已有研究，但双曲型PDE因策略-状态映射的紧性问题（集值约束映射的规则性不足）导致存在性理论长期缺失，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对状态约束凸GNEP与双曲PDE耦合问题，通过处理集值约束映射的规则性障碍，建立存在性理论；并针对一类线性双曲PDE推导一阶最优性条件。

Result: 成功建立了耦合双曲PDE的状态约束凸GNEP的均衡存在性理论，并给出了线性双曲PDE的一阶最优性条件。

Conclusion: 本文解决了双曲型PDE耦合GNEP的存在性难题，所推导的最优性条件适用于波动方程、广告动力学和网络上的线性化等温欧拉系统等重要应用场景。

Abstract: We study the existence of equilibria for state constrained, convex generalized Nash equilibrium problems (GNEPs) coupled with hyperbolic partial differential equations (PDEs). Analogous problems have been addressed for state constrained GNEPs coupled with elliptic and parabolic PDEs, respectively, but the problematic regularity of the set-valued constraint maps has been a barrier for development of an existence theory in the hyperbolic case. This is mainly due to compactness issues with the strategy-to-state maps. Beyond existence, we also provide first-order optimality conditions for a class of fairly general linear hyperbolic PDEs and show its relevance for applications such as the wave equation, advertising dynamics, and the linearized isothermal Euler system on networks.

</details>


### [134] [Closing the Oracle-Complexity Gap in Derivative-Free Convex Optimization: A Near-Quadratic Lower Bound from Exact Function Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13335)
*Phillip Kerger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究仅使用精确函数值最小化凸Lipschitz函数的确定性查询复杂度，在d维欧几里得球上，将下界从Ω(d)提升至Ω(d²/log(d+1))，闭合了自1996年以来的间隙（至多对数因子），并推广到混合整数设定得到Ω̃(d²·2ⁿ)下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决仅使用函数值（无需梯度信息）的凸优化查询复杂度中，精度Θ(d^{-1/2})处存在的Ω(d)下界与O(d²log²d)上界之间的巨大差距，该差距自1996年至今未被解决。

Method: 通过确定性查询复杂度的信息论下界分析，构造坏情况函数族，证明任意确定性算法在达到精度Θ(d^{-1/2})时必须进行至少Ω(d²/log(d+1))次函数值查询；并将该分析技术推广到混合整数凸优化场景。

Result: 在仅使用函数值的设定下，得到查询复杂度下界Ω(d²/log(d+1))，与现有的O(d²log²d)上界相匹配（至多对数因子）；混合整数设定下得到Ω̃(d²·2ⁿ)下界。

Conclusion: 闭合了Protasov值仅方法自1996年以来的复杂度间隙，证明了仅使用函数值的凸优化与使用一阶梯度的设定之间存在本质性维度平方差距，并揭示了混合整数凸优化中离散变量带来的指数级复杂度提升。

Abstract: We study the deterministic query complexity of minimizing a convex Lipschitz function over a $d$-dimensional Euclidean ball using only exact function values. At accuracy $Θ(d^{-1/2})$, the previously applicable lower bound was $Ω(d)$, inherited from the stronger full first-order oracle, while an upper bound from Protasov's value-only method requires $O(d^2\log^2 d)$ evaluations. By providing a lower bound of $Ω(\,\frac{d^2}{\log(d+1)})$ on the oracle complexity in this setting, we thereby close this gap dating back to 1996, up to polylogarithmic factors. Furthermore, we are able to lift this result to the mixed-integer setting: Mixed-integer convex optimization with $d$ continuous and $n$ discrete variables using function values requires $\tildeΩ(d^2\cdot 2^n)$ queries.

</details>


### [135] [Learned Pairwise Deep Dual-Optimal Inequalities for Stabilizing Column Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13373)
*Zhengzhong Ricky You,Bo Tang,Haoran Liu,Baichuan Mo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出L-PDDOIs框架，通过学习预测对偶变量间的成对序关系并将其作为约束加入主问题，以加速列生成收敛。在CVRP和VRPTW上，直接部署使CG时间降低89.7%和93.9%，仅损失1.3%和0.5%的界；恢复过程保持54.8%和83.1%的时间缩减，且保证界无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列生成中不稳定的对偶解会显著降低收敛速度。现有深度对偶最优不等式虽能限制对偶空间以减少不稳定性，但其构造依赖问题特定的交换论证，难以推广到带容量限制、时间窗等资源约束的路径问题中。

Method: 提出L-PDDOIs学习框架：(1) 采样最优对偶解，选取在足够大公共子集上同时成立的成对序关系作为训练标签；(2) 分类器为候选关系打分；(3) 图后处理过滤压缩冲突冗余的候选集；(4) 引入恢复过程，选择性地松弛所学不等式，在恢复基线CG界时提供证书。

Result: 在CVRP和VRPTW主测试集上，直接部署L-PDDOIs使几何平均根CG时间分别降低89.7%和93.9%，平均界损失仅1.3%和0.5%。恢复过程分别保持54.8%和83.1%的时间缩减，且保证CG界无损。

Conclusion: L-PDDOIs框架有效解决了传统深度对偶最优不等式在资源约束路径问题中构造困难的问题，在显著加速列生成收敛的同时通过恢复过程提供了理论保证，为大规模优化算法提供了实用且可靠的加速方案。

Abstract: Column generation (CG) is central to many large-scale optimization algorithms, including branch-price-and-cut methods for vehicle routing problems, but unstable dual solutions can substantially slow its convergence. Existing deep dual-optimal inequalities can reduce this instability by restricting the dual space. Their construction, however, typically relies on problem-specific exchange arguments that are difficult to establish for routing problems with capacity limits, time windows, and other resource constraints. We introduce learned pairwise deep dual-optimal inequalities (L-PDDOIs), a learning framework that predicts pairwise orderings between dual variables and incorporates their primal counterparts directly into the master problem. To construct training labels, the framework samples optimal dual solutions and selects pairwise order relations that hold simultaneously on a sufficiently large common subset of the samples. A classifier then assigns a score to each candidate relation. Because conflicts and redundancies among the predicted relations can impair performance, graph-based postprocessing filters and compresses the candidate set before deployment. We further introduce a recovery procedure that selectively relaxes learned inequalities and provides a certificate when the baseline CG bound has been restored. On the main test sets for the capacitated vehicle routing problem and the vehicle routing problem with time windows, direct deployment of L-PDDOIs reduces the geometric mean root CG time by 89.7% and 93.9%, respectively, while incurring mean bound losses of only 1.3% and 0.5%. The recovery procedure retains corresponding time reductions of 54.8% and 83.1%, respectively, while guaranteeing no loss in the CG bound.

</details>


### [136] [Hager-Zhang Conjugate Gradient Method for Set Optimization with Set-Valued Objective Map of Finite Cardinality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13383)
*Ravi Raushan,Debdas Ghosh,Zai-Yun Peng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于求解集合优化问题的非线性Hager-Zhang共轭梯度法，该方法不依赖于排序锥的有限生成元或最优解处的正则性条件，通过Wolfe线搜索和Drummond-Svaiter标量化函数，证明了算法的良定性、下降方向性和全局收敛性，并通过数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集合优化方法通常对排序锥的有限生成元或最优解处的正则性条件有严格依赖，限制了其应用范围。本文旨在提出一种不受这些限制的数值方法，使其同时适用于集合优化和向量优化问题。

Method: 采用Drummond-Svaiter标量化函数处理Wolfe线搜索条件，引入Hager-Zhang标量共轭参数构造搜索方向，推导了沿下降方向满足Wolfe线搜索条件的步长存在性，建立了Zoutendijk-like条件以实现全局收敛。

Result: 证明了所提方法生成的搜索方向是下降方向，方法的良定性得到验证；建立了类似Zoutendijk条件的全局收敛保证；数值实验表明该方法在多种测试问题上具有良好的实际性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的非线性Hager-Zhang共轭梯度法有效解决了集合优化问题，在无严格约束条件下实现了全局收敛，数值实验验证了其实用性和有效性，对集合优化和向量优化领域均有重要意义。

Abstract: This work introduces a nonlinear Hager-Zhang conjugate gradient method for solving set optimization problems. The objective function under consideration is defined by a finite collection of continuously differentiable functions. Notably, the proposed approach imposes restrictions neither on the existence of a finite generator of the ordering cone nor on any regularity condition at the optimal solution. As a result, the proposed method holds considerable significance for both set optimization and vector optimization problems, with the latter serving as a special case of the former. The study begins by discussing Wolfe line search conditions using Drummond-Svaiter scalarization function. Thereafter, we establish the existence of a step length satisfying the Wolfe line search conditions along a descent direction. The Hager-Zhang scalar conjugate parameter is introduced to derive the search direction for the proposed method. It is established that the direction generated by the proposed method is a descent direction. The well-definedness of the proposed method is given. Furthermore, we discuss some important results and a Zoutendijk-like condition to ensure global convergence. Subsequently, the global convergence of the proposed method is established in an asymptotic manner. Finally, numerical experiments on various test problems validate the practical performance and effectiveness of the proposed technique.

</details>


### [137] [Norm-Cone Conjugation and Fenchel-Type Duality Beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13495)
*Fernando García-Castaño,Miguel Ángel Melguizo-Padial*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于范数锥共轭的非线性对偶框架，通过将仿射minorants替换为平移范数锥，在非凸条件下建立了Fenchel型对偶理论，并给出了次微分刻画和基于摄动法的强对偶条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Fenchel共轭基于仿射minorants，局限于凸分析。为了将共轭对偶理论推广到非凸情况，本文引入范数锥共轭方案，用平移范数锥替代仿射函数作为minorants，建立非凸框架下的对偶理论。

Method: 定义范数锥共轭（由斜率参数α≥0和中心参数x₀索引），对应的双共轭为所有下方范数锥minorants的上确界。引入容许斜率和容许高度概念，证明Fenchel-Young型不等式。定义范数锥次微分，建立与精确支撑和双共轭的关系。最后，基于摄动变量上的部分范数锥共轭，发展摄动对偶理论。

Result: 建立了Fenchel-Young型不等式；刻画了精确双共轭的范数锥可支撑性条件；给出了范数锥次微分与精确支撑和双共轭的关系；证明了弱对偶性无需凸性假设，强对偶性可由度量下界条件（包括一致下Lipschitz估计和价值函数的下平静性）保证。

Conclusion: 该工作将Fenchel共轭对偶理论从凸分析推广到非凸情形，通过范数锥共轭建立了非线性对偶框架，为非凸优化问题提供了新的对偶理论工具。

Abstract: We introduce a norm-cone conjugation scheme for extended-real-valued functions on normed spaces. The construction replaces affine minorants by translated norm-cones of the form \(x\mapsto r-α\|x-x_0\|\), with \(α\ge0\), and establishes a nonlinear conjugation framework underlying a Fenchel-type duality theory beyond convexity. The resulting conjugate is indexed by slopes and centres, and the associated biconjugate is the supremum of all norm-cone minorants lying below the function. We prove Fenchel--Young type inequalities, introduce admissible slopes and admissible heights, and characterize exact biconjugation in terms of norm-cone supportability. We also define a norm-cone subdifferential and relate it to exact support and biconjugation. Finally, we develop an abstract perturbation duality theory based on partial norm-cone conjugation in the perturbation variable. Weak duality holds without convexity assumptions, while strong duality follows from metric lower-bound conditions, including uniform lower Lipschitz estimates and lower calmness of the value function.

</details>


### [138] [Energy decay and weak observability for some evolution systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13502)
*Kaïs Ammari,Faouzi Triki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在抽象泛函框架下证明了耗散算子对应的弱可观测性与能量衰减之间的等价性，统一了稳定性与控制的理论视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究演化方程长时间行为，探究弱可观测性与能量衰减这两个基本性质之间的深层关系。

Method: 利用弱可观测性理论的最新进展（AT1中的量化不等式技术），在适当假设下建立等价性证明。

Result: 证明了在适当假设下，弱可观测性与能量衰减不仅是密切相关的，而且是等价的。

Conclusion: 该等价性为抽象动力系统的稳定性与控制提供了统一视角，弥合了定性衰减性质与谱估计之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: In an abstract functional framework, we investigate the equivalence between two fundamental properties: weak observability and energy decay associated with certain classes of dissipative operators. This analysis is motivated by the study of evolution equations and their long-time behavior. Our main result establishes that under appropriate assumptions, these two notions are not only closely related but in fact equivalent. The proof relies on recent advances in the theory of weak observability, particularly those presented in \cite{AT1}, where new techniques have been developed to quantify the weak observability inequalities. These tools allow us to bridge the gap between qualitative decay properties and spectral estimates, offering a unified perspective on stability and control in abstract dynamical systems.

</details>


### [139] [Infinite horizon stochastic optimal control with sign-changing discount factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13547)
*Charles Bertucci,Jean-Michel Lasry,Pierre-Louis Lions*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程研究无限时域随机最优控制问题，其中折扣因子可依状态取值正负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究折扣因子可正可负（取决于状态值）的无限时域随机最优控制问题，这是传统假设折扣因子恒为正的推广。

Method: 利用Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程分析该问题。

Result: 针对折扣因子可依状态取正负值的特殊情况，给出了相关的理论分析结果。

Conclusion: 扩展了随机最优控制理论，使其适用于折扣因子符号随状态变化的情形。

Abstract: We study an infinite horizon stochastic optimal control problem by means of the associated Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation. The problem we are studying has the particularity of having a discount factor which can take both signs, depending on the value of the state.

</details>


### [140] [The Value of Perfect Endpoint Forecasts for Offshore-Wind Thermal Firming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13627)
*Arash Khojaste,Geoffrey Pritchard,Golbon Zakeri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种仅基于当前和单一未来端点信息（而非完整路径）的短期预测方法在海风热力调峰中的应用，发现单步远端预测的边际价值随预测步长增加而递减，1小时端点预测可降低8.07%的年调峰成本，6小时仅为2.28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测评估仅关注准确性，忽略了信息结构对运行模型决策价值的影响。本文旨在揭示在海上风电热力调峰场景下，仅提供当前状态和单一未来端点（而非完整路径）的预测信息结构，其经济价值随预测视界的变化规律。

Method: 将端点信号嵌入到具有周期平稳性的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中，利用分位数傅里叶回归状态估计，基于ISO新英格兰负荷和海上风电数据求解年度状态-动作频率线性规划（LP），对预测步长h=1至6进行评估。

Result: 1小时端点预测降低年调峰成本8.07%，6小时端点预测仅降低2.28%，边际价值随预测步长增加递减。

Conclusion: 单一远端端点预测对于单步爬坡决策的可操作性随预测步长增加而下降，但这并不意味着更长的滚动路径预测没有价值；信息结构与预测步长对运行决策的影响需要区分对待。

Abstract: Forecast value depends not only on accuracy but also on the information structure available to the operating model. We study a diagnostic current-endpoint-only forecast for offshore-wind thermal firming: at hour t, the controller observes the current net-demand state z_t and one perfect future target z_{t+h}, but not the intermediate path or earlier endpoint messages. Unlike rolling path forecasts, these endpoint information sets are not nested in h. We embed the signal in a cyclostationary MDP using quantile Fourier regression states estimated from ISO New England load and offshore wind data, and solve annual state-action-frequency LPs for h = 1, . . . , 6. A one-hour endpoint forecast reduces annual firming cost by 8.07%, while a six-hour endpoint reduces it by 2.28%. The decreasing profile shows that a single farther endpoint is less actionable for a one-step ramping decision, without implying that longer rolling forecasts are less valuable.

</details>


### [141] [Meeting Uncertain Threats with Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13648)
*Louis L Chen,Ang Xu,Roberto Szechtman,Chiwei Yan,Vince Vanterpool*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对防空反导、海军力量保护及关键基础设施安全中的防御资源分配问题，将其建模为马尔可夫决策过程，研究在威胁清除不确定且仅能在射击回合后观测结果的情况下，如何快速分配有限效应器。主要发现是：忽略威胁难度的公平分配策略在多种目标下表现出色，而考虑威胁难度的贪心策略在异构威胁场景下接近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防空反导、海军力量保护及关键基础设施安全需要快速将稀缺的效应器分配给多个来袭威胁，而中和效果不确定且仅在射击回合后可见，现有方法缺乏高效、可快速实施的分配策略。

Method: 将防御资源分配问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，指挥官每回合将固定效应器容量分配给异构威胁，针对三种操作目标（最小化预期威胁清除时间、最大化截止期限前清除概率、最大化截止期限前的有效分配）进行研究。提出了公平分配策略和基于威胁难度的贪心策略，并进行了理论分析和数值验证。

Result: 公平分配在异构威胁或低容量交战中对于所有三个目标都是最优的；当效应器容量至少随威胁数量线性增长时，其威胁清除时间与最优策略相差最多常数回合数。贪心策略在数值实验中接近最优，且具有常数因子保证。

Conclusion: 公平分配是原理性选择，尤其在威胁难度未知时；贪心策略在异构威胁场景下接近最优，两者均避免了昂贵的全动态优化。

Abstract: Air and missile defense, naval force protection, and critical-infrastructure security require rapid allocation of scarce effectors against multiple incoming threats when neutralization is uncertain and observed only after a firing round. We formulate this defensive-allocation problem as a Markov decision process in which a commander assigns a fixed amount of effector capacity each round to heterogeneous threats under three operational objectives: minimizing expected threat-clearance time, maximizing the probability of clearance by a deadline, and maximizing effective assignments before a deadline. Rather than expensive full dynamic optimization, we study simple time-oblivious policies suited to fast implementation. Fair allocation, which ignores threat difficulty and spreads fire evenly, is highly effective: it is optimal for all three objectives under homogeneous threats or low-capacity engagements. Moreover, when effector capacity scales at least linearly with the number of threats, its threat-clearance time trails that of the optimal policy by at most a constant number of rounds. We further develop threat-difficulty-aware greedy policies for each objective, including a constant-factor guarantee for effective assignment maximization. Numerically, greedy policies are near-optimal across heterogeneous instances, while fair allocation remains a principled choice when threat difficulties are unknown.

</details>


### [142] [The problem of finding the global minimum of a polynomial on a coordinate parallelepiped](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13658)
*V. N. Nefedov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对多项式在坐标平行六面体上的全局最小值问题，提出变量消去算法（VEA），该方法适用于低维度和低次多项式问题，虽理论通用但实际受限于高维时产生的大量高次多项式求根问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多项式在坐标平行六面体等简单结构集合上的全局最小值问题，该问题具有实际应用价值。

Method: 采用作者此前提出的变量消去算法（VEA），通过逐步消元将原优化问题转化为一系列求多项式实根的子问题。

Result: 该方法在理论上适用于任意多项式优化问题，但实际实现中仅对低维度和低次多项式有效，因为高维情形下会产生大量高次多项式的实根确定问题。

Conclusion: VEA算法虽在理论上具有通用性，但实际应用受限于问题规模和多项式次数；对于低维低次问题，该方法能够成功求解具有实际相关性的优化问题。

Abstract: The paper addresses the problem of finding the global minimum of a polynomial on a coordinate parallelepiped or on certain other sets with a simple structure.
  To solve the problem, the Variable Elimination Algorithm for polynomial optimization problems described in the author's previous works is used.
  It should be noted that the proposed method can be of practical significance only for problems of low dimensionality and for polynomials of not very high degree.
  This is due to the fact that, when implementing the VEA, a large number of alternative problems may arise concerning the determination of real roots of polynomials, and these polynomials may have very high degrees.
  Although, in theory, the Variable Elimination Algorithm is applicable to any polynomial optimization problems, there are often practically relevant problems that can be successfully solved using the proposed method.

</details>


### [143] [Lifting-Free Quadratic Sum-Of-Squares Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13701)
*Gabriel F. Machado,Ross Drummond,Morgan Jones*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对二次平方和(QSOS)优化问题中现有凸锥提升方法导致计算瓶颈的问题，本文提出了一种保持原始锥结构的无提升正则化方法，通过添加SOS变量的范数惩罚实现闭式原更更新和凹对偶，采用加速一阶方法高效求解，在约束回归问题上比SCS快40%，且内存仅随等式约束数量线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QSOS优化问题在系统辨识和机器学习中广泛存在，但标准的Schur补和第二阶锥提升方法会扩大锥维度，给内点法带来计算瓶颈。现有方法的计算效率难以满足大规模问题的需求。

Method: 提出一种无提升正则化方法，通过向SOS变量添加范数惩罚项来保持原始锥结构，从而导出了闭式原更更新和一个具有Lipschitz连续梯度的无约束凹对偶问题。采用加速一阶方法（如FISTA）高效最大化该对偶问题，并给出了非渐近收敛性分析。

Result: 在约束回归问题的数值实验中，所提出方法比现有求解器SCS快40%，能够处理比MOSEK更大规模的问题，且内存开销仅随等式约束数量线性增长。

Conclusion: 本文提出的无提升正则化方法有效解决了QSOS优化中锥提升带来的计算瓶颈，兼具理论保证和实践优越性，特别适合大规模约束回归问题。

Abstract: Quadratic Sum-Of-Squares (QSOS) optimization problems appear in system identification and machine learning, but standard Schur-complement and second-order cone liftings enlarge conic dimensions and create computational bottlenecks for interior-point methods. This paper introduces a lifting-free regularization that preserves the original conic structure by adding a norm penalty to SOS variables, yielding closed-form primal updates and an unconstrained, concave dual with Lipschitz-continuous gradient. Accelerated first-order methods efficiently maximize this dual, and convergence analysis shows non-asymptotic recovery of the solution. Numerical experiments on constrained regression problems show the proposed method can be 40\% faster than existing solvers such as SCS and handle larger problems than MOSEK, with memory scaling only in the number of equality constraints.

</details>


### [144] [Polynomial iISS for a class of Timoshenko equations with external disturbances and infinite history memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13715)
*Xinyu Guo,Jun Zheng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在积分输入到状态稳定性（iISS）框架下研究受外部扰动和无限历史记忆影响的Timoshenko方程的鲁棒性，提出仅基于状态范数的多项式iISS（PiISS）新定义，给出Tolksdorf条件下的PiISS充分条件及更强条件下的指数iISS（EiISS）条件，并通过Lyapunov泛函和先验估计建立适定性和稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PiISS定义依赖于状态图范数，与无穷维系统的经典iISS概念不一致，需要给出仅基于状态范数的严格PiISS定义以统一框架。

Method: 利用半群和椭圆方程理论建立适定性；构造合适的Lyapunov泛函并结合多种先验估计来评估PiISS和EiISS。

Result: 在Tolskdorf条件下推导出PiISS的充分条件，在更强条件下推导出EiISS的充分条件，完整刻画了外部扰动和历史记忆对系统稳定性的影响。

Conclusion: 本文为Timoshenko方程在扰动和历史记忆下的稳定性提供了统一的iISS理论框架，克服了图范数定义的不一致性。

Abstract: This paper investigates the robustness of Timoshenko equations subject to external disturbances and infinite history memory within the integral input-to-state stability (iISS) framework. While polynomial iISS (PiISS) is a powerful tool for characterizing the influence of these two factors, the existing definitions of PiISS rely on graph norms of the state, which are inconsistent with the classical iISS notion for infinite-dimensional systems. In this paper, we provide a rigorous PiISS definition for a class of Timoshenko equations solely via the state norm, and derive sufficient conditions for PiISS under the Tolksdorf condition on the relaxation function, as well as exponential iISS (EiISS) under a stronger condition. The well-posedness is established by using the semigroup and elliptic equation theories. The PiISS and EiISS are assessed by constructing appropriate Lyapunov functionals and employing various a priori estimates of solutions, thereby fully characterizing the impact of disturbances and history memory on system stability.

</details>


### [145] [Bayesian Risk Preference Persuasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13810)
*Shutian Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个信息设计（说服）框架，研究如何通过信息设计来影响决策者的风险偏好。接收者初始具有由一致风险度量表示的风险偏好，在观察发送者设计的状态信号后，会以保持时间一致性的方式更新风险偏好，进而改变后续决策和结果。文章刻画了最优信息设计的特征，识别了发送者受益的条件，并通过再保险设计示例展示了框架在风险管理中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策者的风险偏好并非固定不变，而是会随着外部信息动态调整。这一事实在经典风险决策理论中未被充分考虑，因此有必要建立一个理论框架来解释和利用这种偏好变动性。

Method: 建立了基于一致风险度量的说服模型：接收者初始持有风险偏好，发送者设计信息规则生成系统状态信号，接收者观察状态后以保持时间一致性的方式修订风险偏好。通过分析后验信念的诱导分布来刻画发送者的最优信息设计，推导不同情境下信息设计有益于发送者的条件。

Result: 刻画了发送者最优信息设计的特征；识别了信息设计能够使发送者受益的条件；通过再保险设计应用展示了框架的实际价值。

Conclusion: 通过信息设计可以主动引导决策者的风险偏好调整，而保持时间一致性的约束是可行设计的关键。该框架为风险管理等领域提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: A decision-maker's risk preference is inherently unstable and may adjust in response to external information, shaping subsequent choices and outcomes. This paper develops a persuasion framework to study how information can be designed to steer risk preferences and decision results. In our model, a receiver starts with an initial risk preference represented by a coherent risk measure and revises it after observing a system state generated by an information rule claimed by a sender. The revision must preserve time consistency of risk evaluations before and after the state realization. We characterize the sender's optimal information design by analyzing the induced distribution of posterior beliefs over states. Each belief leads to specific preference revisions and corresponding conditional risk assessments. We identify conditions under which information design benefits the sender across several settings and illustrate the framework's potential in risk management through an application to reinsurance design.

</details>


### [146] [Associated gradients: connection to conservative fields and application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13973)
*Cheik Traoré*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明分段光滑局部Lipschitz函数的关联梯度是保守场的选取，其集合沿Lipschitz曲线满足链式法则，从而调和了非光滑自动微分的两个理论框架，并证明了随机次梯度方法在保守和Clarke临界点处的迭代收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和非光滑自动微分中两个存在差异的理论框架（Clarke次微分与保守场理论），并证明分段光滑局部Lipschitz函数的关联梯度天然满足保守场性质。

Method: 首先证明关联梯度构成一个满足链式法则的集合（保守场），进而指出Clarke次微分作为其凸子集也继承该性质；随后在随机次梯度方法框架下，基于有界性假设证明迭代点列收敛到保守临界点和Clarke临界点，并保证函数值收敛。

Result: 1) 关联梯度集合是保守场；2) Clarke次微分继承链式法则；3) 证明了新一类路径可微函数；4) 随机次梯度方法在关联梯度驱动下，迭代点列收敛到保守和Clarke临界点，函数值收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上统一了非光滑自动微分的不同框架，并为使用关联梯度的随机优化算法提供了收敛性保证。

Abstract: In this paper, we demonstrate that the gradient associated to a representation of a locally Lipschitz, piecewise-smooth function is a selection of a conservative field. Indeed, we prove that a well-defined set, containing these associated gradients, has the chain rule property along Lipschitz curves. Consequently, the Clarke subdifferential, as a subset of its convex hull, also inherits this property for these functions. Ultimately, this work reconciles two theoretical frameworks that address notably nonsmooth automatic differentiation. As a byproduct, it also proves a new class of path differentiable functions. From an algorithmic perspective, under a boundedness assumption, we provide the subsequence convergence of the iterates to both conservative and Clarke critical points, as well as the convergence of the function values, for the stochastic subgradient method, where associated gradients are used to drive the dynamic.

</details>
